iḷaṅkumaraṉār
tamiḻvaḷam
27
1. kaḻaka āṭciyar va.cu.varalāṟu

āciriyar :
mutu muṉivar irā.iḷaṅkumaraṉ
vaḷavaṉ patippakam
ceṉṉi - 600 017.


nūṟ kuṟippu
nūṟpeyar	:	iḷaṅkumaraṉār - tamiḻvaḷam 27
āciriyar	:	irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ
patippāḷar	:	kō. iḷavaḻakaṉ
patippu	:	2013
tāḷ	:	16 ki veḷḷittāḷ
aḷavu	:	1/8 temmi
eḻuttu	:	11 puḷḷi
pakkam	:	16 + 368 = 384
nūl kaṭṭamippu	:	iyalpu (cātāraṇam)
vili	:	urupā. 240/-
paṭikaḷ	:	1000
nūlākkam	:	cevaṉtceṉ kamyūṉikēcaṉ

veḷiyīṭu
vaḷavaṉ patippakam
eṇ : 2 ciṅkāra vēlar teru,
tiyākarāyar nakar, ceṉṉi - 600 017.
tolipēci : 24339030



patippuri
ilakkiya ilakkaṇac cemmal mutumuṉivar ayyā iḷaṅkumaraṉār avarkaḷ eḻupattintu āṇṭu niṟivu eytiyatip pōṟṟum vakiyil tamiḻ iḷiyarkkeṉṟu avar eḻutiya nūlkaḷiyellām cērttu eḻupattintu nūlkaḷ kaṭanta 2005 - ām āṇṭu tiruccit tirunakaril veḷiyiṭṭu em patippup paṇikkup perumi cērttuk koṇṭōm. 
ipperuntamiḻ aṟiñar eḻutiya paṭippukaḷi 20 tokutikaḷāka (1 mutal 20 vari) avariṉ 81-ām āṇṭu niṟiviyoṭṭi kaṭanta 2009 ām āṇṭil veḷiyiṭṭup perumi cērttōm. perumikkup perumi cērkkum vakiyil avar tam aṟivu viḷiccali 21 mutal 40 vari 
20 tokutikaḷi  83-ām āṇṭu (2012) niṟiviyoṭṭi tamiḻulakam payaṉkoṇṭu ceḻikkum vakiyil veḷiyiṭukiṟōm. avar tam aruntamiḻp paṇiyi vaṇaṅki makiḻkiṟōm.
ipperumakaṉ vāḻum kālattilēyē ivar eḻutiya eḻuttukkaḷ, pēciya pēccukkaḷ aṉittiyum tokuttu veḷiyiṭuvatu eṉpatu tamiḻnūl patippu varalāṟṟil pōṟṟi makiḻvataṟkāṉa,  aṉivarum piṉpaṟṟuvataṟkāṉa arumperum tamiḻp paṇiyākum.
ivar tannalam karutāmal tamiḻ nalam kāttu varupavar.  tammimuṉṉiṟuttātu  tamiḻi muṉṉiṟuttum peruntamiḻaṟiñar.  tamakkeṉa vāḻātu tamiḻkkeṉa vāḻpavar. orunāḷiṉ muḻup
poḻutum tamiḻākavē vāḻum tamiḻp pōrāḷi. caṅkac cāṉṟōr variciyil vittu eṇṇattakka aruntamiḻaṟiñar. tamiḻ ilakkaṇa - ilakkiya marapik kāttu varum marapu vaḻi aṟiñar. āravāram mikunta iṉṟiya cūḻniliyil paṭāṭōpam iṉṟiyum, viḷamparap pōlimi iṉṟiyum,   tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ āḻa akalaṅkaḷi akaḻntu kāṭṭum toltamiḻaṟiñar. moḻinūl katiravaṉ pāvāṇariṉ vērccol āyvil avar kāṭṭiya vaḻiyil tam tamiḻāyvit  toṭarpavar. 
tamiḻāyvuk kaḷattil  tam āyvuppayaṇattit toṭaṅkum tamiḻ āyvāḷarkaḷukkum, tamiḻ uṇarvāḷarkaḷukkum, māṇavac celvaṅkaḷukkum taṅkat taṭṭil vittup poṟ
kuviyalākat  tantuḷḷōm. em tamiḻ nūl patippup payaṇattil,  tamiḻtteṉṟal tiru.vi.ka.viyum, taṉittamiḻiyakkat tanti maṟimaliyaṭikaḷiyum, moḻinūl katiravaṉ pāvāṇariyum, tamiḻ moḻikkāvalar ilakkuvaṉāriyum vaḻikāṭṭiyākak koṇṭu tamiḻnūl parappiṉ elliyik kaṇṭu kāṭṭum caṅkattamiḻc cāṉṟōrāka viḷaṅkum aiyā iḷaṅkumaraṉār  vāḻum kālattilēyē avar eḻutiya nūlkaḷi veḷiyiṭuvati yām peṟṟa pēṟākak karutukiṟōm. tamiḻar illamtōṟum irukkattakka ivvaruntamiḻc celvaṅkaḷit tamiḻ iḷam talimuṟikku vippākak koṭuttuḷḷōm. 
 caluki pōṉāl pōkaṭṭum - eṉ 
	aluval pōṉāl pōkaṭṭum 
	talimuṟi orukōṭi kaṇṭa - eṉ
	tamiḻ viṭutali ākaṭṭum.
pāvēntar varikaḷi niṉivil koḷvōm.
- patippāḷar.




peṟum pēṟu
vaḷam eṅkē irukkiṟatu?
vaḷam eṅkēyum irukkiṟatu! aḵtu illāta iṭamē illi! 
vaḷam eṅkum iruppati eppaṭi aṟiyalām?
vaḷattit tēṭum uḷam iruntāl, vaḷam eṅkēyum iruppati aṟiyalām?
vaḷattiṉ aḷavu eṉṉa?
vaḷattiṉ aḷavum uḷattiṉ aḷavēyām!
uḷattil vaḷam illiyāṉāl, uḷḷa vaḷamum uṭiyavaṉukkum utavātu ūrukkum ulakukkum utavātu!
vaḷattit tarum uḷatti oruvar vāykkap peṟṟāl pōtum!
avvaḷam kuṉṟiyaḷavāṉāl eṉṉa?
     kuṉṟattaṉiya vaḷamāṉāl eṉṉa?
     tiṉi aḷavāṉāl eṉṉa?
     paṉi aḷavāṉāl eṉṉa?
     vaḷam vaḷamē!
tēṉī vaḷam? tiṉittaṉi aḷavup pūvil, tēṉī koḷḷum tēṉ evvaḷavu irukkum?
kōttumpi koṇarum tēṉ vaḷam evvaḷavu irukkum?
kōttumpi koṇarvatē vaḷam, tēṉī koṇarvatu vaḷamilli eṉa evarāvatu kūṟuvārā?
vaḷam vaḷamē! avaravar uḻippu, aṟivuttiṟam, uṇmiyaṟivu, paṭṭaṟivu, kāla-iṭa-cūḻal koṭi- ākiyavaṟṟip poṟuttatu atu.
poruḷ maṭṭumā vaḷam?
pulami vaḷamilliyā?
ūkkutal utavutal uṟutuṇiyātal oṉṟutal āyavi - vaḷamilliyā?
ellām vaḷamē! mallal vaḷaṉē eṉpatu tolkāppiyam. (uri.7) uṭalālum vaḷam; uḻippālum vaḷam; uṇarvālum vaḷam; uriyālum vaḷam; vaḷam paṭa vāḻa vēṇṭātār evar? vaḷam peṟa vēṇṭātār evar?
vaḷantali mayaṅkiya vañci muṟṟam
eṉpatu cilampu! vaḷattiṉ aḷavu, aḷantu kaṭi aṟiyāvaḷam eṉpatu (25:33-34)
nāṭeṉpa nāṭā vaḷattaṉa
eṉpatu nāṭṭiṉ ilakkaṇam! eḷimi, iṉimi, iṟimi, niṟimi eṉa makkaḷ vāḻnta nāḷ ilakkaṇam! iṉṟō,
nāṉila vaḷaṅkaḷiyum nāṭā nāṭilli!
enta nāṭu ēṟṟumati ceyyavilli?
enta nāṭu iṟakkumati ceyyavilli?
pālivaḷatti nāṭiccōli niṟṟal veḷippaṭi eṉṟāl, colvāṉēṉ?
nāṭāvaḷattil kuppikkīri cērātā? kuṟuntūṟu cērātā?
vaḷatti vaḻaṅkutal tāṉ piṟavik kaṭaṉē yaṉṟi aḷaviṭum poṟuppu vaḻaṅkuvārkku illi! peṟuvār poṟuppu. āṉāl,
vaḷam peṟṟup peṟṟup peruki vāḻntavar, avvaḷattit tamakku vaḻaṅkiya maṇṇukku maṟittu vaḻaṅkip peṟṟa kaṭaṉit tīrkkāmal pōṉāl kaṭaṉkārarākavē pōvatu maṭṭumaṉṟi, piṟavi aṭiyāḷamē illāmalum pōyviṭuvār!
peṟṟavar vaḻiyākak karuvilē peṟṟa tiru eṉṉa! kaṟṟa kalviyāl peṟṟa vaḷam eṉṉa! paṭṭaṟivāl - āḻmaṉa āyvāl - tēṭik koṇṭa vaḷaṅkaḷ eṉṉa eṉṉa! piñcup paruva mutal perum pirivu vari peṟṟavaṟṟi, annilipparuvattarkkup paruvamaḻi pōlap palappala vakiyālum vaḻaṅkutal vāḻvār vāḻvuc cīrmiyātal vēṇṭum!
purivu terinta nāḷ mutal pulami perukivarum aḷavukkut takat-takat tolkāppiyar āṇi vaḻi,
ōytal āytal niḻattal cāay
         āvayiṉ nāṉkum uḷḷataṉ nuṇukkam
eṉṉum nālneṟik kaṭaṉkaḷiyum niṟivēṟṟal vēṇṭum! kūṟṟiyum āṭal koḷḷum koḷki ituveṉa uṇarvār ōyār, oḻiyār; cāyār; carintum pōkār!
eṉ iḷantip paruvamē, āciriyap paruvamākiviṭṭatu.  akkālac cūḻal atu.
piṟantanāḷ : 30.01.1930
āciriyap paṇi ēṟṟa nāḷ: 08.04.1946
kāl caṭṭi pōṭum māṇavapparuvattil vēṭṭi kaṭṭum āciriyaṉāki viṭṭēṉ! mūṉṟāṇṭu āciriyap paṇi ceytāl pōtum! pulavar tērvit taṉiyāka eḻutalām eṉṉum vāyppu! avvāyppic cikkeṉap paṟṟiyamiyāl 1949, 1950, 1951 ākiya mūvāṇṭukaḷil toṭarntu nuḻivut tērvu, pulavar iṭinilit tērvu, pulavar niṟi nilit tērvu eṉac ciṟappuṟa veṟṟi vāyppu eytiyatu.  uyarpaḷḷi, mēlpaḷḷi eṉpavaṟṟilum palkalik kaḻakat tamiḻiyal āyvuk kaḷattilumāka ēṟattāḻa 43 āṇṭukaḷ tamiḻvāḻvāka vāḻa vāyttatu.  tamiḻttoṇṭum, tamiḻneṟit toṇṭum eṉṟum viṭutal aṟiyā viruppoṭu ceytu koṇṭiruttalē iyaṟkiyōṭu iyinta vāḻvāki viṭṭatu!
pulamit tērvukkuriya pāṭattaḷavilō, nūlkaḷiṉ aḷavilō eṉ kalvi niṉṟatu illi! tolkāppiyam mutal iṟṟināḷ nūlkaḷ vari iyaṉṟa aḷavāl tamiḻvaḷam peṟutali nōṉpākak koṇṭu kaṟkum pēṟupeṟṟēṉ.  pērāciriyar ci.ilakkuvaṉār, moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇar, nāvalar ca.cō.pāratiyār, varalāṟṟuc cemmal aracamāṇikkaṉār, pintamiḻp pāvalar m.ki.guªjhkdh®, urivēntar auvi cu.turicāmiyār, mūtaṟiñar cemmal va,cupa.māṇikkaṉār, tāmaric celvar va,cuppiyaṉār, īrōṭu vēlā, kuḻittali mī.cu. iḷamuruku poṟcelvi, patippuc cemmal meyyappaṉār, pārati cō.cāminātaṉār, tamiḻppōrāḷi iḷavaḻakaṉār, tamiḻ mīṭpukkut taliniṉṟa āṉārūṉā, pāvāṇar aṟakkaṭṭaḷiyar kōvalaṅkaṇṇaṉār, maliyaka māriyappaṉār iṉṉar nerukkat toṭarpukaḷ vāyttaṉa.
iḷantip paruvattilēyē pāvēntar, ōki cuttāṉanta pāratiyār, kavimaṇiyār, kavirāca paṇṭita cekavīra
pāṇṭiyaṉār, aṟiñar mu.va. āyōr ariya kāṭciyum ciṉmuṟic cantippukaḷum eṉṉuḷ pacumi nalkiṉa. 
tamiḻp poḻivukaḷum, āyvuk kaṭṭurikaḷum, kuṭumpac caṭaṅkukaḷum eṉ mīṭṭeṭuppup paṇikaḷ āyiṉa!
em kuṭumpak kaṭamikaḷ em paṇiyiyō toṇṭiyō kavarntu koḷḷā vakiyil iṉiya tuṇiyum makkaḷum amintaṉar.
ataṉāl, kālamellām kaṟkavum kaṟpikkavum nūl
paṭikkavum vāytta vāyppup peritāyiṟṟu.
aṉpum aṟivum eṉṉum nūl toṭaṅkic centamiḻc coṟporuḷ kaḷañciyam eṉat toṭarntu ēṟattāḻa 400 nūlkaḷ iyaṟṟavum veḷiyiṭavum vāyttaṉa.
kuḻanti nūlkaḷ, nūlaka nūlkaḷ, āyvu nūlkaḷ, akarātikaḷ, urinūlkaḷ, patippu nūlkaḷ, pā nūlkaḷ, kāppiyaṅkaḷ, katikaḷ, kaṭṭurikaḷ, tokuppukaḷ ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷ eṉap palappala tiṟattaṉavāyp pāl vāyp paruvattar mutal palkalik kaḻaka āyvar varikkum payaṉ koḷḷum vakiyil avi amintaṉa.
eti eṭuttālum pāṭalākkal orukālam; katiyum nāṭakamum ākkal orukālam; patippup paṇiyē oru kālam; uri
kāṇalē oru kālam; paṭippaṭiyē collāyvē vāḻveṉa ākiviṭṭa kālam eṉa amintaṉa.
ik koṭikaḷ vaḻaṅkiyavi pārati patippakam, civacittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakam, maṇi patippakam, murukaṉ patippakam, vēlā patippakam, maṇivācakar patippakam, vēmaṉ patippakam, mūvēntar patippakam, pāvāṇar aṟakkaṭṭaḷi, cākittiya akātemi, ulakat tamiḻ ārāycci niṟuvaṉam, tamiḻmaṇ patippakam,  amiḻtam patippakam, vaḷavaṉ patippakam, māṇavar patippakam, amiḻtamaṇi patippakam, tiruvaḷḷuvar tavaccāli aṟakkaṭṭaḷi veḷiyīṭukaḷ eṉa virintaṉa.
nūlurup peṟṟu accaka vaḻiyum patippaka vaḻiyum kāṇappeṟāmalum etirpārā maṟivukaḷālum ēṟattāḻac ciṟitum peritumāy oḻintavi irupatukku mēl āyiṉa.
centamiḻ, centamiḻc celvi, tamiḻppāvi, kuṟaḷ
neṟi, kuṟaḷiyam, malarkaḷ, āyvaraṅkaṅkaḷ eṉak kaṭṭurikaḷ perukiṉa.  accukku varātaviyum ākiṉa!  ivi eṉṟum orumukamāyk kiṭikka vāyppilli.
em nūlkaḷ emmiṭamē muppatukkumēl illāmal maṟintaṉa.  eḻutiyaṉa ellāvaṟṟiyum orumukamākap peṟap
palar avāviṉār. tavattiru āttumāṉanta aṭikaḷār (āruyirkku aṉpar) atil talippaṭṭu niṉṟār.  āyar āṇṭaki cūcimāṇikkaṉār cuṭarēṟṟiṉār. pāratitācaṉ palkalikkaḻakam matippuṟu mutumuṉivar paṭṭam vaḻaṅkiyatu.  ataṟku makiḻvuṟṟa vakiyāl pāvāṇar tamiḻiyakkam mutalām amippukaḷum, muṉivar tirumāṟaṉār mutalām ārvalarkaḷum viḻā oṉṟu eṭuttaṉar.  avviḻāt talimiyit tiruttaku tuṇivēntar poṉṉavikkō ēṟṟār.  tavattiru āttumāṉanta aṭikaḷār vāḻtturittār.  poṟiñar tamiḻaṟiñar pālakaṅkātaraṉār eṉ nūlkaḷ cilavaṟṟi oḷippaṭiyākki uyaraṭṭik kaṭṭil vaḻaṅki avaṟṟi muḻutuṟap peṟa mīḷacciṭutal vēṇṭum eṉṟār.  tamiḻākkap patippē vāḻvāka oṉṟiya iḷavaḻakaṉār muḻutuṟa acciṭa oppiṉār.  aṭikaḷār veḷiyīṭṭup poṟuppu ēṟpatākak kūṟiṉār. kiṭikkum nūlkaḷ tavirttup piṟavaṟṟiyellām acciṭum tiṭṭam koṇṭu ēṟattāḻa irupatu, irupatu tokutikaḷāka veḷiyiṭuvatu eṉa uṟutippaṭuttap paṭṭatu.  avvakiyil vāyppatē, iḷaṅkumaraṉār tamiḻvaḷam eṉṉum it toki nūlkaḷām! it tokiyil viṭupaṭum nūlkaḷ kaṭṭurikaḷ, pāṭalkaḷ palapala;  tokukka vāykkā vakiyil kiṭṭāvakiyil maṟappu vakiyil viṭupāṭuṭiyavi uḷa.  eṉiṉum toṇṇuṟu viḻukkāṭēṉum oru mukamākap peṟum pēṟu vāykkiṉṟatu eṉpatu makiḻvūṭṭuvatām!
makkaḷ vāḻvu vaḷarniliyatu! nūlkaḷum vaḷar niliyavi! ātalāl oruvāḻvuk koṭi maṭṭumaṉṟu ītu; oruvāḻvu peṟat takka palparuvak koṭiyumām eṉal cālum! itaṉ aṭippaṭiyām ettēṭalilum peritum tōyāmal tamiḻvaḷat tēṭalilēyē kālamellām cōmpalic cuṭṭerittu, iṭukkaṇukku iṭukkaṇ ākki vāḻnta vāḻvuk koṭiyām itu!
ivvaḷatti muḻutuṟa eṇṇiṉ oṉṟu uṟutiyāka veḷippaṭum.  nalavāḻvu nalvāḻvu karutāta - iyaṟki iṟimip potumi karutāta - eppaṭippum empaṭippil irā eṉpatēyām.  ivaṟṟip payilvār peṟum pēṟum atuvāka amiyiṉ appēṟē yām peṟum pēṟām.
ivvaḷam veḷivara virumpiṉōr uḻittōr utaviṉōr āya aṉivarkkum naṉṟik kaṭappāṭuṭiyēṉ! vāḻiya nalaṉē! vāḻiya nilaṉē!
nellukku umiyuṇṭu
nīrukku nuriyuṇṭu
pullitaḻ pūvukkum uṇṭu
kuṟikaḷintu niṟi peytu
kaṟṟal, kaṟpōr kaṭaṉeṉal muntiyar muṟi
iṉpa aṉpuṭaṉ,
irā.iḷaṅkumaraṉ

maturi kāmarācar palkalikkaḻakam
tuṇivēntar, ṭākṭar. va.cupa.māṇikkam avarkaḷ aṉpuṭaṉ aḷitta


aṇinturi

tāmaric celvar tamiḻp perumakaṉ cuppiyā piḷḷi varalāṟu taṉiyoruvar varalāṟaṉṟu, nūṟṟitaḻt tāmari pōlat tamiḻmoḻik kāppiyum vaḷarcciyiyum perumiyiyum pulamiyiyum taṉṉuṭkoṇṭa māperu varalāṟākum. taṉit tamiḻiyakkam, intiyetirppu iyakkam, tamiḻicik kiḷarcci, tamiḻ vaḻipāṭu, tamiḻāṭci, tūya tamiḻitaḻ, accakam, nūlniliyam, tamiḻk kalikaḷ, pulavar viḻā, pulavarip pōṟṟal eṉpaṉavellām ivar varalāṟṟoṭu toṭarpuṭiyavi.
periyavar va. cuppiyā piḷḷi tamiḻvaḷarccikkut tāmē kaṇṭa patippu neṟikaḷum nūṉmuṟikaḷum talippukaḷum viyakkattakkavi. coṟpoḻivu eṉṟa nūl vakiyil caṅka mutal tamiḻilakkiyam ellām kālattiṟkēṟṟa kivaṭivu peṟṟaṉa, nūṟṟukku mēlāṉa aṟiñarkaḷiṉ matinuṭpattiyum eḻuttu vaṉmiyiyum tiruvuruvaṅkaḷiyum coṟpoḻivu eṉṉum ivvuri naṭic ciṟṟilakkiyam potintu vittiruk kiṉṟatu. etirkāla āyñarkku ivi toṭumaṇaṟ kēṇiyākum. caṅka ilakkiyap pēḻi eṉpatu tamiḻukkut taṅka illap pēḻi eṉpataṉi varalāṟu collum. kuḻaviyar, ciṟār, iḷiñar, mutiyōr, peṇṭir, ayalōr, māṟṟu moḻiyiṉar eṉa aṉitiṟattiṉarkkum pakkuva nūlkaḷ veḷiyiṭṭa pār pukaḻ tamiḻttoṇṭu tiruvaraṅkaṉār tōṟṟiya va. cuppiyā piḷḷi nilināṭṭiya kaḻakattiṟkē uriyatu.
maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ nūlniliyam eṉpatu vilimatikka muṭiyāta aruṅkali māḷikiyākum. tāmariccelvar tam muyaṟciyāl tamiḻaṉṉikkuk kaṭṭiya tamiḻaṇi niliyam eṉṟu ulakaṟiñar pārāṭṭukiṉṟaṉar. evvaḷavu iḷiya aṟivuṭi nampikaḷ inniliyattiṟku nāḷtoṟum ceṉṟu iruntu kuṟipput tokuttu āyvi vitikkiṉṟaṉar. iḵtu aṟivāṟṟuppaṭiyāka viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu.
etirmaṟiyākap pārttāṟṟāṉ meyt toṇṭukaḷiṉ ēṟṟaṅkaḷ pulappaṭum. civacittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakam eṉa ōr amippu illātiruntāl, kaḻakap pāṭanūlkaḷ illāti runtāl, caṅkavilakkiyaṅkaṭkum, kāppiyaṅkaṭkum, tolkāppiya mutalāṉa ilakkaṇaṅkaṭkum ciṟṟilakkiyaṅkaṭkum, urip patippukkaḷum, mēlurip patippukkaḷum, aṭakkap patippuk kaḷum, urinaṭip patippukkaḷum illātiruntāl, mu.va.viṉ teḷivuri, tirukkuṟaḷ nāṭ kuṟippu, tiruvaḷḷuvar malar mutalāṉa malarkaḷ, moḻi peyarppukaḷ, pulavar varalāṟukaḷ, aṟiviyaṟ kottukkaḷ, vaṭamoḻi tamiḻakara varicikaḷ, centamiḻccelvi illātiruntāl, malikaḷiṟ ciṟanta tamiḻmaliyāṉa maṟimaliyaṭikaḷiṉ kaṭalaṉiya nūlniliyam illātiruntāl, ittuṇikkum viṉimutalvarāṉa tiruvaraṅkaṉāriṉ iḷavalāṉa tāmaric celvariṉ teyva vuṇarvum, kuṟikkōḷ vīramum, nīṭiya āyuḷum illātiruntāl, irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭut tamiḻaṉṉi oru kuḻanti tūkkic cellum melluruvamāka iruntiruppapāḷ. tāmarip piḷḷiyum kaḻakamum tōṉṟi vaḷarntu āṟṟiya uḻavut toṇṭiṉāl nam tamiḻaṉṉi talipalavum, kikāl plavum peṟṟu nāṭi narampellām nūlmuṟukkēṟik kuruti aṟivoḷi peruki, tici nāṉkum ōṭi, aintu kōṭi makkaḷum pattukkōṭit tōḷil cemmāntu tūkkumaḷavu uyirvaḷam taḻittirukkiṉṟāḷ. ippukaḻcci perumikiyaṉṟu.
toṇṭāṟpaḻutta tamiḻk kiḻavar va. cuppiyāpiḷḷi varalāṟṟiṉit toyyā naṭiyil murukupaṭa eḻutiyuḷḷār pulavar iḷaṅkumaraṉār. pakutit talippukaḷ ilakkiya maṇaṅ kamaḻkiṉṟaṉa. kāla muṟiyākac ceytip puṉiviṉṟiyum naṭippuṉivōṭum iv varalāṟu amintuḷḷatu. tāmaric celvariṉ vāḻvu virivi aṟintār innūli ōr aṭakkap patippākavē karutuvār. eṉiṉum im mutaṟ patippu putiya ceytikaḷ pala potinta mūlappatippākum.
iv varalāṟṟip paṭippār, tamiḻip paṭippār, tamiḻt toṇṭu ceyvār, toṇṭāḷarkaḷi matippār, tamiḻp piṟappi maṉaṅ koḷvār. tamiḻt toṇṭiṉāl tamakkum oru varalāṟu varumpaṭi tamiḻvāḻvu naṭattuvār. ituvē tāmaric celvar tamiḻarpāl etirpārkkum taṉṉalam.


FOREWORD
Dr. K.R.SRINIVASA IYENGAR
Retd. Vice-Chancellor
Andhra University.
Former Vice - President
Sathithya Akademi, Delhi.
It is with pleasure that I Write this Foreword to Pulavar 
R. Ilankumaran’s detiled account of the life-work and times of Tiru Va Subbiah Pillu, whose name has become almost synonymous with the Personality, drive and achievements of the Tirunelveli South India Saiva Siddhanta Book- Publishing Kazhagam and its several affiliated instittuions. I have been a distant admirer of Tiru Va. Su for many years, and since my boyhood years in Tirunelveli. I have watched the Saiva Siddhanta Book-Publishing Society grow from its early auspicious beginings to its prsent impressive strength and healthy wide-ranging influence. I therefore welcome this opportunity to offer my own tribute to the Man and the Kaxhagam he has fostered with such care and clear sense of direction.
Dr. Johnson’s words “slow rises worth by poverty depressed” had a close application to the first phase of Tiru Va Su’s life and career, Tlhe fourth son of Vairamuthu Pilli and Sundarathammayar of Palayamkotti, Subbiah losthis father when barely three, and two of his brothers and his only sister before he was twenty-five. On his elder brother, Tiruvaranganar, senior by seven years, fell the responsibility of sustining the family throught its difficult years. He found employment in a commercial establishment in Colombo in 1907, and during the next ten years, his monthly remittance were the minstay of the family. In the meantime, Subbiah passed his S.S.L.C. in 1916 from St. Xavier’s High School, Palayamkotti, and proceeded to Colombo to continue his studies. But not long after, illness made him return to Palayamkotti, however, he duly passed the London Matric in 1917 as a private candidate. Failing to secure admissin to the Madras Medical College in 1918, Va Su groped about for a while, till he presently found his great mission in life as salesman of the nectar of Tamil letters.
During his sojourn inColombo, Va Su’s brother, Tiru varanganar, made the acquaintance of the notred Tamil scholar and writer, Marimali Adigal, arranged for the savant’s memorable first visit to Colombo in 1914, and stimulated the sale of his books. It was during his second visit in 1917 that Va Su met the Adigal andlid the foundation of his Boswellian relationship to the Master. That year also saw the establishment by Tiruvaranganar of Tiru - Sankar Company, a bookshop in Colimbo for the sale of the works of Marimali Adigal and other scholars. Tiruvaranganar, however, Soon realised that his real work lay inTamil Nadu, and hence moved to Coral Merchant Street, Madras, to organise the bookshop Tiru-Sankar Company, while asking Va Su to open in the family home at Palayamkotti a branch of the firm. Tiruvaranganar and Va Su firmly believed that the sale of good books, being verily the commerce of idean and knowledge and values., blessed the givers and takers alkie; no surfeit in the receiving, and no loss in the giving!
Tlhe next decisinve turn was the incorporation by the brothers, on 21 September 1920, of the Tirunelveli South India Saiva Siddhanta Book-publishing Kazhagam. The brother received  much encouragement from Tiru M. Diraviyam Pilli and Tiru T.S. Visvanatha Pilli, and the Kazhagam commenced its career with and authorised capital of Rs. 50,000, made up of 5000 shares, each of Rs. 10. The capital was to be increased in 1952 to Rs. 1 lakh, and in 1963 to Rs. 2 lakhs. With the opening of the Madras Branch of the Kazhagam in February 1921, with Va Su as Manager on Rs. 35 per month (Tiruvaranganar shifting to the Tirunelveli Head Office), the Tirunelveli - Madras axis startedfunctioning with quiet efficiency and a change of missinary zeal.
The new publishing firm was active from the very beginning. The bookshop Tiru - Sankar Company. and the Saiva Siddhanta Book-publishing Corporation teamed together and merged, and in 1923 a Tamil monthly literary journal, ‘Senthamil Selvi’, waslunched as an auciliary force pointing towards the same end. In book-publishing, the new firm aimed at the diffusionof religious (especially Saiva), ethical, and worthy secular literature, classical as well as modern, and worthy secular literature, classicalas well as modern, and also at promoting a two-way Suez Canal-like traffic in letters:: Tamil into other languages,and Singnificant literature in other languages (notably English) into Tamil. There was to the a stress also on Scholarship, exegesis research, literary criticism and interpretaton. The first book to be issued under the Kazhagam imprint was, appropriately enough, Tiruvadi Pugazhamali, and while the first year saw the publication of five books, the 2nd saw seven, the 3rdthriteen, the 4th twenty, and so on. Forty years after, In 1961, the 1008th book came out, and today  the total in 1750 or more, averaging 25 per year, or one bookfor every 13 days during the entire 60-year period of the Kazhagam’s existence.
Tiruvaranganar at Tirunelveli and Va Su at Madrasconstituted between them a resourceful and energetic publishing duumvirate for about 25 years, from 1920 to 1944. Good book-publishing is a taxing and trcky vocation that calls for a rare union of secular and spiritual qualities. A book has a soul as well as a body, aham and not alone puram it calls for the mobilisation of several instruments of production. Buildings, printing facilities, finance, editorial expertise,talent for marketing. pleasing and effective public relations, and above all a flir for the location of writing talent and its mobilisation :: all are needed. AndTiruvaranganar and Va. Su. were able to assemble a dedicated group of writers andeditors, and publish a body of literature (minly in Tamil), both impressive in bulk and marked by a varegated richness. Tiruvaranganar’s marriage to Marimali Adigal’s dughter, Nilambiki, in 1927, and Va Su’s to Mangayarkarasi (dughterof Tiru Aundiappa Pilli of Palayamkotti), in 1935 brought a humanising influence to the brothers’ lives; and Tiruvaranganar in Tirunelvaly and Va Su in Madras worked in perfect co-ordination devoted to the clims of the hearth and equally to the exaction demands of book-publishing as well as advancing the other aims of the Kazhagam.
The untimely death of Tiruvaranganar, th Father of the Kazhagam, in 1944, followed by that of Nilambiki Ammal next year, was a double blow under which the publishing firm could not but reel for a time. It now became necessary for Va Su’s wife, Mangayakarasi, to shift to Palayamkotti with her two dughters, so as to be able to look after Tiruvaranganar;s children and the aged mother-in-law., During the next eight years, Va Su gallan-tly contined at Madras, cookming his own meals, and living a life of simplicity, austerity and infallible regularity. And he laboured with redoubled dedication and saw that the progress of the Kazhagam didn’t suffer,whether in Madras or at Tirunelveli, He bought the Janasakthi Press for Rs.20,000, re named it Appar Achakam, and had it opened by Omandur Ramaswami Reddiar in 1948. This gave a push to the Kazhagam’s book-publishing activities and helped it to build an opulently diversified body of publications in a variety of formats;; mini, medium and large, paperback, calico, rexine,popular, and gold-lettered deluxe! learned commentaries on Tolappiyam, and other works of grammar;; the whole ensemble of the Sangam Classics;; the minor but no less significant literary forms (ula, kalambakam, thoothu, Kuravanji, andadi, pillithamizh etc.) ;; the celebrated Tirukkural in a diversity of editons, one of them (with Mu Varadarjanar’s paraphrase) achieving a sale of a million;; the classics of religions divination and ecastasy like Tirumandiram, Tevaram, Tiruvasagam and Tiruppugazh;; philosophical classics like Meikandar’s;; the Sangam Classics in prose or in summary reecitals;; collections of speeches and conference papers;; historical and scientific works;; secular literature like fiction, essays and drama; children’s literature; the works of Marimali Adigal; translations; research monographs in Tamil and in English; biographies, dictionaries..........well, God’s plenty, in fact. The Kazhagam’s list is a composite and comprehensive library by itself. No wonder it has been remarked that the Kazhagam, as an instittution, is older than the Annamali,and Maduri KamarajUniversities; and its record of scholoarly publications in Tamil is more outstanding than that of the University of Mazdras.
In September 1950, the death of Marimali Adigal left a big void in theTamil world. By his last will and testament, he hadbequeathed his very considerable privare library of rare Tamil books and other scholarly works to the Kazhagam, and this priceless collection of about 4000 books was to become the necleusof the Marimali Adigal Memorial Library, which was duly opened by Tiru. M. Bhaktavatsalam on 24th August 1958, in a historic building in Linghi Chetti Street, The building has seminal associations with Ramalinga Vallalar, who is sid to have delivered his rirst impromptu discourse there to and astonished assembly. The Library began with the 4000 of the Marimali Adigal collection and anothe 5000 in the Kazhagam’s garner. Since then there have been steady accessions, includiug whole libraries like that of the historian,the late V. Rangachari; and today the library has about 35,000 books and rare manuscripts. Although located in a crowded part of the city, the Library is the hunt of Tamil scholars old and yound; and, for the committed, it is what anideal Library shouldbe, a Houseof Perpetual Enlightenment. I may be added that Marimali Adigal’s ownhouse in Pallavaramhas also been turned by the Kazhagaminto a memorial Kali Manram, and is serving the cuse of arts and letters during the last two decades.
What becomes clear when lone reads Pulavar Ilan kumaran’s account of the life of Tiru.Va. Subbiah Pili is that the biography merges with the history of a dedicated family, as also with that of Kazhagamand its collateral institutions, and even with that of the Pure Tamil movement and the Tamil literary renissance witnessed during the last sixty years. Tiruvaranganar and Va Su were closely linkedwith Marimali Adigal as disciples and heirs to his ministry in the service of Tamil. If Tiruvaranganar became the Adigal’s son-in-law also, on Va Su fell the responsibility of cherishing and fostering the Marimali tradiontgs of integrity and high seriousness in Tamil scholarship. After performing the marriage of Tiruvaranganar’s dughters, Mayilamami and Muthammal, Va Su thought of the marriageof his own dughters Vadivazhagiar and Vairamaniyar. The former was married in 1956 to Kalyansundaram, Va Su’s brother-in-law’s elder son. The younger son, Mutukumaraswamy married Vairamani in 1959. And in yound Muthukumaraswamy - young but fully trined in Library Science-the Marimali Adigal Library has found a seasoned libratian,who has fosteredits growth in the right directions andmade it a veritable mecca for advanced Tamil studies and research.
Between 1920,the year of the Kazhagam’s foundation, and today over sixty years have passed, For the first 25 years, the Tiruvarangam-Va Su duumvirate gave the right leadership to the Kazhagam. After 1944, althoughg Va Suhad to take upon himself the responsibilities at Madrasa swell as at Tirunelveli, he had good collaborators, and found in his sons-in-law admirable executants. At Tirunelveli, the Sivagnana Munivar’s Library, the Thiruvalluvar Tamil College and the Kumaraguruparar Nursery School came up one after another. Branches of the Kazhagam have been established at Maduri, Coimbatore, Kumbakonam and Tiruchirappalli. Although never in exceptionally robust health (he had a period of acute illness at Jaffna in 1951), Va Su has both kept going the different institutions under his charge in a high pitch of efficiency and also constantly endeavoured to add new dimensins to their activities. He is a master builder, really:: he is a Kazhagam by himself, and always a power for good.
The incorporation of the Saiva Siddhanta Book-Publishing Foundation was an act of faith, a blow struck in favour of Tamil language, literature and culture. Like Marimali Adigal,the Kazhagam and Va Su have been advocation with evangelical fervour the ‘Pure Tami’ movement, it was Suryanarayena Sastri who first changed his Sanskritised name into the Tamil ‘Parithimar Kalignar’ Likewise ‘Swami Vedachalam’ underwent a sea-change into ‘<arimali Adigal’. In the pase there had doubtles been a priod of heavy and sometimes humourless Sanskritisation of Tamil  with its native, homely and beautiful usages. This corresponded to the Latinisation, in mediaveval times, of English (or Anglo-Saxon) forms. In the 19th century, however,there was a determined move in England to prefer always the Saxon to the more recondite or polysyllabic Latin word. But this too some times led to absurdities, and in course of time, after the swong between thesis and antithesis, the English language has been trying to arrive at a sensible, intelligible and lively synthesis, with  a broad baseof unity that nevertheless permits surface variations depending on the locality, occasion or speaker, In Tamil Nadu, agin.,the earlier Sanskritisation and the ,ore recent Anglicisation have tended to obscure the native hue and force and beautyy of Tamil, and the determined effort to return to easy and natural Tamil usage is certinly to be welcomed. On the other hand, linguistic ‘purism’ - in any extremely narrow sense of the term - is no virtue, and in the modern world teeming with new ideas, siturations and technologies, can even hamper the natural growth of a language. In hisintroduction to my Literature and Authorship in India (1943), E.M<. Forster described himself as a ‘convinced impurist”, implying that linguistic purism should not be pursued at the expense of intelligibility and the dynamic of linguistic growth in a fast-changing world. While without question there should be broadly understood norms of spoken and written language, a single adamantiune- law cannot be made to apply to all speakers and users of a language, pursuing as they may diversoccupatons, and cughtin all kinds of situations.
The Kazhagam’s and Tiru Va Su’s meritorious efforst to safeguard the sovereign interests of Tamil and to resist aggression by otehr languages (Hindi, for example) areworthy of all prise, but I also feel that within a broad framework of unity and uniformity, a certin play of variation in individual and situational usage is inevitable, and will be a signof healthy growth, And great and ancient and uniquely rich as is the Tamil language, it cannot quite ignore the need to build bridgesof understanding with the other languages of India, the classical Sanskrit and the living regional languages.
It is worth mentioning also that the Kazhagam, under Va Su’s sleepless stewardship, has organised conferencesand Tamil Vizhas (festivals) to emplore the richesof Tamil leters and popularise the values and verities of the literature, are and cultureof the Tamil country. Thus there have been almost 20 conferences (at Tirunelveli, Madras and Maduri) on classics like pathuppattu, Nattrini, Ettuthohi, Kalithohi, Purananooru, Theshorter epics and so on, and the lecturesgiven and the papers presented have been collected and published by the Kazhagam. As for the festivals, there was the first Tiruvalluvar Vizha at Pachayappa’s College in 1935, amemorable event. Othershave follwed, theannual Marimali Adigal festivals, periodic festivals for book-releases or in commemoration of particular poetes and literary celebrities, seminarson select themes, the festival in 1977 in connection with Senbthamil SElvi’s golden junbilee - these and other vixhas, carefully planned and organish\ed with every attention to detil by Va Su and his collegues, hav \e done their best ot keep the Flame of Tamil letters and culture alive and aloft.
Aside from his manifold activities directed towards the realisations of the far aims of the Kazhagam and its connected institutions, VA Suis knownin his own right as a writer, especially of books for children. He wrote for his children first, and his books have since become a legacy for all children, Besides, the kazhagam has sponsored the publication of about 300 books for children. For Va Su the past is his foundation, the present the arena of activity, and the future the threatre of his dreams and hopes and plans. He was one of the spirits behind the Tamil Protection SAngam lunched in 1937, and he has outlined his 21-point programmed for the conservation and development of Tamil. With his uncanny flir for recognising and enlisting writing talent for engineering effectively the renissance in Tamil letters, he has been able to build the Kazhagam’s massive and may- sided publishing list. He takes a midwife’s personal interestin almost every book that appears over the Kazhagam imprint, and he is sid to attend to proof-reading even today.
And in sun, how shale we take the measure of the man VA Su or of his work spread over 60 years and in so many directions? Pulavar Illankumaran has gathered together a number of tributes, in prose and in verse, to VA Su by a galaxy of his contemporaries, admirers and friends. A dutiful son, brother, householder and patriarch of the family; a man of ceaselessindustrylabbouring always unmindful of reward and unafrid of possible failure; a man with no need for vacations; a Karmayogi with skill in works and a will to do his best, and every waiting on Grace; the lover of all loversof Tamil, without distinction of caste, creedor positon; editor of Senthamil Selvi for 55 years; a publisher with a rare knowledgeof the instruments he has to deploy, and with a talent for choosing the right people for the work on hand; a leader evoking loyalty all roundand eliciting their fullest co-operation; a visionary with a feelong for the future and its paramount needs; ‘Padma Sri’ and ‘Senthamil Selvar;; stuch adherent of aram, or dharma: such has been, and such is, Tiru VA Su, who has sustined himself woith the Kazhagam’s ideals and inspirations, and also sustined the Kazhagam with his very life-breath. Agent, Secretary Managing  Director, the designations haven’t mattered mu;ch to him, for total dedication has been the sole criterion of his sevice. While these achievem,ents undert the Kazhagam’s banner have been phenomenal enough, Va Suis without pride, and he is far from complacent. He knows that there is still a long way to go. He is a man of simple food hits, wedded to simplicity and austerity; and he can readily forget and forgive an injury. He abhors waste, idleness and gossip; and, deep within, he is strong with the strength of his religious faith and reliance on the Grace Divine. Admirers have hiled him as a university in hikself, and as a waling library, but these desfcriptions canot exhust the Manor his work. He lightly carries the weight of his 84 years, and he has not still ceasedto hanker afterfurther goals of aspiration and achievement. He is indeed the Happy Warrior who has made his life a grand God-given opportunity for unselfish unblemished and unending service to Tamil. On this occasion I wish his many more years of active and useful life, and all joy of fulfilment in the new tasks that he may undertake.
152, Kutchery Road,	K.R.SRINIVASA IIYENGAR
Mylapore, Madras : 600004
24th August, 1981

	kōṭu kōṭāyt tōṉṟum koḷkittē
eṉṉum cilappatikāram toṭarpāṉa paḻam pāṭaṟ kuṟippu iv varalāṟṟukkum ēṟkum!
tamiḻaṉṉiyiṉ nilimakkaḷuḷ talimakkaḷākat tōṉṟi yōr palar; avarkaḷ paṭippāḷar; uriyāḷar; patippāḷar; parappāḷar; kāppāḷar eṉap pala tiṟattar. aṉṉa vari ellām tamiḻkūṟu nallulakap parappellām iṉṉār eṉṟu oppārum mikkārum illā niliyila parappik koṇṭirukkum ēntal iv varalāṟuṭiyār!
teruvellām tamiḻmuḻakkam ceḻikkac ceyvīr eṉṟum, tēmaturat tamiḻōci ulakamelām paravumpaki ceytal vēṇṭum eṉṟum, tiṟamāṉa pulamiyeṉil veḷināṭṭār ati vaṇakkam ceytal vēṇṭum eṉṟum uṇarvup paṭippāḷikaḷ kaṭṭaḷiyiṭutal eḷitu. āṉāl avaṟṟi uṇarntu uruppaṭiyāyk kaṭamipuriyum ceyalvīrar kāṇṭaṟku ariyar; mika mika ariyar! ivvariyarāyc ceyaṟkariya ceyyum periyarāy, aṟupāṉ āṇṭukaḷukku mēlum taṭippāṭum iṭippāṭum iṉṟit toṭarntu ceyalāṟṟik koṇṭuvarum uravōr ivvaralāṟṟu nāyakar eṉpatu nāṭaṟinta ceyti!
coṟkōvum tōṇipurat tōṉṟalumnam cuntaraṉum
	ciṟkōla vātavūrtti tēcikaṉum - muṟkōli
	vantilarēl nīṟeṅkē? māmaṟinūl tāṉeṅkē?
	entipiraṉ ainteḻutteṅ kē
eṉṟu civāṉanta māli eṅkē? eṅkē? eṅkē? eṉa viṉā eḻuppukiṉṟatu. iv varalāṟṟu nāyakar tōṉṟāviṭiṉ, tiruvaraṅkaṉārkkup patippuk kātal kiḷarntu kaḻakak kaṉi mara vittu ūṉṟātiruntirukkappeṟiṉ, ettaṉi ettaṉi eṅkēk kaḷik kēṭpatu?
kaḻaka nūlāciriyarkaḷ ōriṭattār allar; ōriṉattār allar; oru kālattār allar; oru tuṟiyār allar; oru niliyār allar; oru camayattār allar; oru pālār allar; ivarum, oru nūṟṟuvar irunūṟṟuvarallar; paṉṉūṟṟuvar! ik kaḻaka nūlāciriyarkaḷ maṭṭumō ivar toṭarpaḷār?
viḻumiya viḻāt talimiyēṟṟōr ettuṇir? poḻivu ceytu polivuṟuttiyōr ettuṇiyar? aluval toṭarpāl aṇukkarāyōr ettuṇiyar? araciyal vallunar, nītittuṟic cāṉṟōr, kalvit tuṟi, kalit tuṟi cārntēr - ivarkaḷ ettuṇiyar? ivarkaḷ aṉivar nerukkamum nēyamum oṉṟuviṭāmal eḻutap pukiṉ iv varalāṟu ettuṇi virivām?
orō oru nūl veḷiyīṭṭilēyē tokkik kiṭakkum varalāṟukaḷ ettuṇiyō uṇṭu! orō oru nūlāciriyar toṭarpilēyē ettuṇiyō varalāṟṟuk kuṟippukaḷ tokkik kiṭappatu uṇṭu! ivvāṟāka ēṟakkuṟiya 1725 nūlkaḷi veḷiyiṭṭa veḷiyīṭṭu vēntariṉ varalāṟṟil, veḷiyiṭṭa oru nūlukku oru pakkam eḻutuvatu eṉṟālum, innūl evvaḷavu virintu pōkum! oru pāṉi cōṟṟukku ōraviḻ patam eṉṟu iv varalāṟṟik koḷka.
tokuppuk kali ēntalākiya ivar, kaṭitakkalik kāvalar eṉpatik kaṭṭāyam kuṟippiṭal vēṇṭuma. ivar eḻutiya kaṭitaṅkaḷ ōrāyiramā? īrāyiramā? paṉṉāyiram eṉka. ik kaṭitaṅkaḷuṅku vippup paṭikaḷ uṇṭu eṉṟāl, ivar kaṭita vippar eṉpatileṉṉē aiyam! ivar eḻutiya kaṭitaṅkaḷ ittuṇi eṉṟāl, ivarukku aṟiñarkaḷ eḻutiya kaṭitaṅkaḷ ettuṇi! kaṭṭuk kaṭṭākavum, kōppuk kōppākavum vikkappeṟṟuḷa! avi āvaṇak kāppakam pōlak kaṭitak kāppakamāka amittaṟku uriyavi. avaṟṟi oḻuṅkuṟutti virinta varalāṟu eḻutuvatāyiṉ āyiram āyiram pakkamāka nūl viriyum. avvirinta nūl, tamiḻakattiṉ ivvirupatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ varalāṟṟuk kalik karuvūlamākat tikaḻum!
eḷiyēṉ vāḻvil iyalpākap peṟṟa ariya pēṟukaḷ cila uṇṭu. avaṟṟuḷ taliyāya oṉṟu kaḻaka āṭciyāḷar avarkaḷiṉ kaṇipparumkaṭṭilāk kātaṉmit toṭarpu! at toṭarpāl aminta perumpēṟu, eḷiyēṉ vāḻvu, muḻutuṟum amiḻtat tamiḻ vāḻvu ākiyatēyām! iṉpat tamiḻil iṭiyaṟātu tōyntu, eyppilā nalatti eṉṟeṉṟum eytikkoṇṭirukkum pēṟu eḷitil evarkkum vāyppatō? av vāyppiṉ iṭiyē vāytta taṉipperum pēṟu - ceyalāṟṟutalic ceḻuntavamākak koṇṭa ic centamiḻc celvar varalāṟṟi eḻutum pēṟākum. eṉṉeṉiṉ, iv varalāṟuṭiyār iṭattiliruntu tatumpi vaḻiyum taṉittamiḻp paṟṟu evarkkē vāykkum? it taṉit tamiḻt toṇṭu ceyya ettuṇip pēr muṉintār? am muṉippi eṉṟum ayarāmal cōrāmal muḻutuṟac celutti ettuṇip pēr ceyalāṟṟiṉār? ivartam ceyalāṇmit tiṟattiṟku it tuṟiyil ivarkku oppākavātal, uyar vākavātal evarē iruntār? ivarip pōl payaṉila niṉiyāmi, payaṉila collāmi, payaṉilu ceyyāmi evarē pārittuc cikkeṉap pōṟṟiyiruntār? ivarippōl payaṉila evarē pārittuc cikkeṉap pōṟṟiyiruntār? ivarip pōl maṟimalikkup parappunarākavum, kūṭṭuppaṅku niṟuvaṉat tiṟkuk kāṭṭunarākavum evarē iruntār? irukkiṉṟār?
paṭam pārttal, veṟṟili pōṭutal, pukipuṭittal, kāppi tēnīr ākiyavi kuṭittal, iḻinta pāṭalkaḷik kēṭṭal, takavaṟṟa nūlkaḷip paṭittal - ākiyavi ivar kaṉaviṉum karutātavi!
cīṭṭu viḷiyāṭutal, paricuccīṭṭu naṭattutal, kutirip pantayattiṟkup pōtal, ēmāṟṟu āṭṭaṅkaḷil īṭupaṭutal ākiyavi ivarāl veṟuttu otukkappaṭupavi!
vīṇ pēccup pēcutal, vāḷā poḻutik kaḻittal, kēlikiṇṭal niyāṇṭi ceytal ākiyavaṟṟil eṉṟum īṭupaṭṭaṟiyār! takkavar toṭarpi aṉṟip piṟar toṭarpi valakkāramāka vilakkuvār! vāṭṭum nōyiṉ iṭiyēyum, ūṭṭum maruntiṉ iṭiyēyum ōyviṉṟi uḻippati oru peruntavamākak koṇṭuḷḷa takavuṭiyār! takuti vāynta paṇikaḷittērntu ceytālvāḻum nāḷum nīḷum eṉpatip palappala cāṉṟukaḷālniṟuvik kāṭṭit tam vāḻviyum avvaṇṇamē uyttuc celvatāl eṉṟum iḷiyarāy vāḻum peṟṟiyār! ic cīrmikaḷi niṉintu niṉintu uṇarntu varalāṟu eḻutum pōtu, anniṉivālum av vuṇarvālum āṭkoḷḷap peṟṟuc cārntaṉaṉ vaṇṇamāka aḻuntippōkum pēṟṟiṉum ariya pēṟu oruvarukku vēṇṭumō? attaku uyirūtiyap pēṟu tiruvaruḷ aruḷiyatē eṉpatu oru tali!
nūṟṟitaḻ alari niri kaṇṭāṟpōla, nūṟu pallāṇṭu uriyum pāṭṭum uṭiyōrāy vāḻkatil eṉpatu māṇikka moḻi! ām; muṉivar va. cupa. māṇikkaṉār ivarkku uritta vāḻttu moḻi. am moḻiyāl moḻi vaḷarkkum ēntali vāḻtti iv varalāṟṟup paṭiyali aṭiyuṟi ceytu makiḻkiṉṟēṉ.
celvam	tamiḻttoṇṭaṉ
tirunakar,	irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ
maturi-6	30-7-81.



1. oru peruviḻā
(paṉinīr palamuṟi teḷittār; cantaṉam aḷittār; māli cūṭṭi makiḻntār; pārāṭṭeṭuttup paravaca muṟṟār; avaṟṟiyellām oruṅkē tirumpappeṟa vāyttavēḷiyil tikkumukkāṭip pōṉār.)
ceṉṉiyilē oru peruviḻā. avviḻāvil araciyal talivarkaḷum aṟiñar perumakkaḷum kuḻumiyuḷḷaṉar. viḻā nāyakarukkup pukaḻmāli cūṭṭukiṉṟaṉar.
eṉakkut terinta variyil tiru.va.cuppiyāpiḷḷi avarkaḷik kolampacukku oppiṭalām. kolampacu amerikkāvik kaṇṭupiṭittatupōl ivarmaṉitaṉik kaṇṭupiṭittār. tamiḻ nāṭṭil yār yārmaṉitarāka irukki ṉṟārkaḷ, eḻuttāḷarkaḷāka irukkiṉṟārkaḷ, aṟiñarāka irukkiṉṟārkaḷ, evarellām nūlāciriyarāka irukkat takutiyuḷḷavarāka irukkiṉṟārkaḷ eṉṟu kaṇṭupiṭittārkaḷ. āciriyarkaḷāka illātavarkaḷi āciriyarkaḷākkiṉārkaḷ. nūlāciriyar ākkiṉārkaḷ.
oru pācuvel illiyāṉal eppaṭi cāṉcaṉ illiyō apōl mṟimaliyaṭikaḷukkup perum viḷamparattit tēṭit tantavarkaḷ tiru. cuppiyā piḷḷi avarkaḷē.
tiru. cuppiyāpiḷḷi avarkaḷ oru puttaka vaṇikar maṭṭumallar. avar oru centamiḻk kāvalar, pulavar, puravalar, paltuṟi aṟivu parappunar: in nāṭṭu muṉṉēṟṟat toṇṭar kaḷuḷ oruvar.
avar ōr iyalpāṉa puttaka veḷiyīṭṭāḷar maṭṭu mallar; avariṭattil irukkum oruperum ciṟappu eṉṉa veṉṟāl, avarē oru ciṟanta eḻuttāḷar. avarē oru puttaka viṟpaṉiyāḷar; avarē oru puttaka veḷiyīṭṭāḷar; avarē oru nūlakar; avarē oru vācakar; ōr itaḻāciriyarākavum irukkiṟār.
aimpatu āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ inta niliyam tuvaṅkum poḻutu enta aḷavukkut taṉittamiḻppaṟṟāḷarāka iruntārō, antat taṉittamiḻp paṟṟil iruntu koñcam kūṭap piṟaḻntu kalapput tamiḻukku avar māṟavilli. aimpatu āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉāl inta niliyattiṉ poṟuppi ivar ēṟkumpōtu eppaṭit tammi oppaṭittārō, ataṟkup piṉpu entak kavarcciyum, enta atircciyum tammi māṟṟaviṭāmal itaṟkē tammi oppaṭittuviṭṭār. ippaṭi oruvar iruppatu inta irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ nākarikatti mīṟiya ceyal
aimpatu āṇṭukaḷil ivariviṭac ciṟanta toṇṭāṟ ṟiyavarkaḷ irukka muṭiyumā?
iṟivaṉ avarukkuk koṭuttirukkum nalla paṇpu, avarukku yārum pakiyilli.
oruvaṉ entac camayattil iruntālum avaṉ tamiḻukku aṉpuṭiyavaṉāka iruntāl avaṉic civaṉ eṉṟu karutuvārkaḷ.
tamiḻ paṭittavarkaḷ tamiḻ uṇarntavarkaḷ yārāka iruntālum nammuṭiya piḷḷi avarkaḷip pōlattāṉ irukka vēṇṭum.
viḻā nāyakar avarkaḷē, oru nalla nūl; anta nalla nūlukku uḷḷa poruḷ tamiḻ oṉṟutāṉ
avarkaḷ tokuttu vittuḷḷa nūl ovvoṉṟum oru kati collum
matippukkuriya nūl niliyaṅkaḷē, niṟintirukkum uṅkaḷ pēḻikaḷil illāta, āṉāl uṅkaḷukku mika mikat tēviyāṉavaṟṟi nāṉēkoṇṭu varukiṟēṉ eṉṟāṉ vālṭṭu viṭmaṉ. antak kaviñaṉip poṟuttavari aḵtu uṇmiyō? poyyō? nāṉaṟiyēṉ; āṉāl tamiḻaṉṉi
yiṉ eḷiya nūlniliyattil illāta, āṉāl iṉṟiyami
yātu tēvippaṭṭa eṇṇaṟṟa nūlkaḷit tiru. piḷḷi
yavarkaḷ koṇṭu tantārkaḷ
tamiḻ vāḻa vāḻnta umatu tavatti iṉṟiya tamiḻakam uṇarntu pōṟṟukiṟatu. etirkālat tamiḻakam niṉintu naṉṟi celuttum; tamiḻ varalāṟu umatu toṇṭiṟkut tali vaṇaṅkum.
piḷḷi avarkaḷiṉ toṇṭiṉip paṟṟik kūṟa vēṇṭu māṉāl avi poṉṉeḻuttukkaḷāl poṟikkat takkavi eṉṟē kūṟavēṇṭum.
avarkaḷ ceytavarum tamiḻttoṇṭu etirkālattil kalveṭṭukkaḷil poṟikku maḷavukkup perumiyuṭiya
tākum.
uṇmiyilēyē civacittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakum eṉṟāl cuppiyāpiḷḷitām. cuppiyāpiḷḷi eṉṟāl civa cittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakamtāṉ.
oru ciṟappāṉa tuṇiyi āṇṭavaṉ avarukku aḷittirukkiṟār.
- ivvāṟu aṟiñar palartāmaric celvar va. cuppiyā piḷḷi avarkaṭkup pukaḻāram cūṭṭukiṉṟaṉar.
viḻā nāyakar eḻupattintāṇṭu iḷiñar (1973 ām āṇṭil) ēṟpuri vaḻaṅkukiṉṟār. appoḻutu teṟitta maṇikaḷ;
nāṉ eppōtum iḷamiyittāṉ virumpuvēṉ
eṉṉi yārāvatu mutiyavar eṉṟu coṉṉāl ati nāṉ cuvippatilli
tamiḻnāṭṭu varalāṟṟi eḻuta vēṇṭumāṉāl - tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ varalāṟṟi eḻuta vēṇṭumāṉāl - eṉ tamiyaṉār tiruvaraṅkar varalāṟṟittāṉ mutaṉ mutalil eḻuta vēṇṭum. eṉ tamiyaṉār illāviṭṭāl maṟimaliyaṭikaḷāriṉ perumi ivvaḷavu viḷakka muṟṟirukkātu eṉpati nāṉ aṟivēṉ.
tamiḻukku eṉṉa ceyyalām, tamiḻarkku varukiṟa iṭiyūṟu kaḷi eppaṭip pōkkalām - iviyē eṉakku eppōtum cintaṉi.
ōyvu oḻiccal eṉpavi eṉakkup piṭikkātavi
eṉakkuk kiṭikkappeṟum pukaḻukkum perumikkum uriyavarkaḷ eṉ arumit tamiyaṉārtiruvaraṅkaṉār avarkaḷum, taṉit tamiḻt tanti maṟimaliyaṭikaḷārum āvar
eṉakku viḻā eṭuppati nāṉ virumpuvatilli. āṉāl inta viḻā ēṉ eṭukkappaṭṭatu eṉṟāl, nāṉ eppaṭiyellām perumpulavarkaḷōṭu toṭarpu koṇṭēṉ eṉpatu avar kaḷukkut teriyavēṇṭum eṉpataṟkākavē. nūlāciriyarkaḷuk kellām eḻuti avarkaḷ karutti aṟiyacceytu ataṉ mūlam avarkaḷum eṉṉippōl perumakkaḷuṭiya aṉpiyum ātaraviyum peṟavēṇṭum eṉpataṟkākavum, eṉṉippōl avarkaḷum ūkkam peṟavēṇṭum eṉpataṟkākavumē iv viḻā eṭukkappaṭṭatē aṉṟi eṉatu perumiyit terivippa taṟkāka aṉṟu.
ip poḻivu maṇikaḷum, ēṟpuri maṇikaḷum mēlvarum varalāṟṟiṉ mēlvaric caṭṭam pōlvaṉavām.


2. oru maṉitar
(oruvaṉ taṉ tōḷilēyē taṉ piḷḷiyi vittuk koṇṭu ataṉit terut teruvākat tēṭiṉāṉ. atupōl ulakam maṉitaṉit taṉṉarukil vittuk koṇṭē maṉitaṉit tēṭi eṅkeṅkō alikiṉṟatu.) 
paṭṭappakalilē oruvar tam talimēl viḷakkit tāṅkik koṇṭu vantār. teruvilpōkiṉṟavarum varukiṉṟa varum, pakalilē viḷakkēntippōkum intap pittiyattip pār eṉap pēci nakittaṉar. oruvar periyavarē, pakalilē ēṉ viḷakkēntikkoṇṭu alikiṟīr? eṉa viṉāviṉār. nāṉ maṉitaṉittēṭi alikiṟēṉ eṉṟār anta maṉitar. tayācīṉacu eṉpatu avar peyar. maṉitari maṉitar tēṭuvatum, maṉitarimaṉitar kaṇṭu koṇṭāṭuvatum ulakil kāṇum nikaḻccikaḷē.
viḷakkēntikkoṇṭu vītiyilē alintu maṉitarit tēṭiyatu mēlināṭu. tamiḻnāṭu, tiṇṇiyilēyiruntu maṉitarik kaṇṭu kaḷippuṟṟatu.
tiruvoṟṟiyūril tēraṭit teruttiṇṇiyil iruntār oru tuṟavi. āṅku iruntukoṇṭu, pōkum makkaḷip pārttu avaravar taṉmikkut takkavāṟu atu pōkiṟatu, itu pōkiṟatu eṉṟu kūṟikkoṇṭiruntār. av vaḻiyāka oru nāḷ vaḷḷalār varak kaṇṭār tuṟavi; paṇivōṭu eḻuntār; uyarntamaṉitar varukiṉṟār eṉṟār. pēraruḷ perukkamē ōruruvākit toṇṭilē tulaṅkum vaḷḷalārē uyarnta maṉitar eṉak koṇṭār, at tuṟavi. maṉitari maṉitar kaṇṭu koṇṭār.
maṉitappiṟavi eṭuttālum maṉitarākak kāṭciyaḷit
tālummaṉitattaṉmi illatavarum ulakil vāḻtal kaṇkūṭu. tiruvaḷḷuvar ittakiyavarkaḷi, makkaḷē pōlvar kayavar eṉṟār. pāratiyār, vēṭikki maṉitar eṉṟār. av vēṭikki maṉitar iyalpukaḷi avar virittuk kūṟiṉār.
tēṭic cōṟunitam tiṉṟu - pala
		ciṉṉañ ciṟukatikaḷ pēci - maṉam
	vāṭit tuṉpamika uḻaṉṟu - piṟar
		vāṭap palaceyalkaḷ ceytu - nari
	kūṭik kiḻapparuvam eytik - koṭuṅ
		kūṟṟuk kiriyeṉappiṉ māyum - pala
	vēṭikki maṉitar.
aritil ellām aritām māṉiṭappiṟaviyi ūṇukkum, veṭṭip poḻutu pōkkukkum, veṟukkum ceyalkaḷukkumē urimiyākkiyavar vēṭikki maṉitar eṉpatil viyappilliyē! atē poḻutil, eḷḷittaṉippoḻutum payaṉiṉṟi irātu, tiṭṭamiṭṭa vakiyilē tiruttoṇṭu ceypavar maṉitaruḷ maṉitar eṉpatilum viyappilliyē!
maṉitaruḷ maṉitarāka oruvari ākkupavar yār? avarē tammi ākkikkoḷḷal vēṇṭum. nalla niliyilē tammi niṟuttupavarum, anniliyilē iruntutammit tāḻttupavarum, tām irukkum niliyilē iruntu, mēlum mēlum uyarttik koṇṭu pōpavarum, evarkkum talimiyāṉavarākat tammic ceypavarum tāmēyaṉṟip piṟarallar eṉap pēcum oru nālaṭippāṭṭu. innili evarkku uṇṭām? tirumūlar itaṟkut teḷivāṉa viṭi pakarkiṟār :
eṉṉi naṉṟāka iṟivaṉ paṭittaṉaṉ
	taṉṉi naṉṟākat tamiḻceyu māṟē
eṉpatu atu.
iṟivaṉ paṭippiṉ nōkkattip purintukoṇṭu, itaṟkākavē eṉṉip paṭittāṉ; avviṟivaṉ paṭippiṉ nōkkatti eṉṉāl evvaḷavu iyalumō avvaḷavu aritiṉ muyaṉṟu niṟivēṟṟutalē eṉ piṟavippayaṉ eṉakkoṇṭu pāṭupaṭupavarē paṇpaṭṭa maṉitar! ittakiyavariyē viyattuḷ vāḻvāṅku vāḻpavar eṉpār tiruvaḷḷuvar.
perumi mikka maṉitarāka vāḻpavar evarō avar, tām piṟanta maṇṇukkum, tām piṟanta iṉattukkum, tām irunta kālattukkum, tām ceyyum toḻilukkum perumi cērkkiṉṟaṉar. itkiyōr perumiyē, etirkāla māntarāl eṭuttukkāṭṭākak koḷḷum ēṟṟam peṟukiṟatu. ivarkaḷ ceyta toṇṭu elli kaṭanta āyiram āyiram āṇṭu kaḷukkum eṇṇippārppatāyp payaṉpaṭuvatāy irukkum. ivvakiyil, tamiḻmoḻiyum, tamiḻnāṭum, tamiḻ iṉamum uḷḷavari uḷḷavarāy vāḻum perumiyit tammōṭu cērttukkoṇṭa peruntaki tāmaric celvar
va. cuppiyāpiḷḷi āvar. tamiḻp palkalik kaḻakam oṉṟit tōṟṟuvikkāta tamiḻakat tīyūḻukku iṭiyē nallūḻāka vāyttavar at tiruvāḷar! avar vāḻvārāka! maṉitarit tēṭit tēṭikkaṇṭu, mūliyil iruntāri muṟṟattil vitta cālap periya rām avar neṭitūḻi vāḻvārāka!

3. perumakaṉār piṟantār
(piṟakkumpōtē maruvum maṇakkum; marukkoḻuntum maṇakkum; tuḷaciyum maṇakkum; āṉāl, tuḷaci maṇamperukku vatuṭaṉ vaḻipaṭu poruḷākavum perumi peṟṟuviṭukiṟatu.)
tamiḻccollukkut taṉicciṟappu uṇṭu. ovvoru colliyum uṉṉippāka nōkkiṉāl, aḵtu oru varalāṟṟu ōviyamāka viḷaṅkum. makaṉ makaḷ eṉṉum coṟkaḷ ac coṟkuṭiyār uyara uyara māṉ, māḷ eṉa uyarntu viṭukiṉṟatu. perumakaṉ perumāṉ ākiṉṟāṉ. perumakaḷ perumāḷ ākiṉṟāḷ! peṇperumāḷ vēṇmāḷ mutaliya peṇpāṟ peyarkaḷi aṟivōr perumāḷ eṉpatu peṇpāṟ peyar eṉpati aiyuṟār:
akal āl ākavilliyā? akaṉṟa pulamiyum, akaṉṟa uḷamum uṭiya akaṉṟōr āṉṟōr allarō! ivvāṟē perumakaṉ perumāṉ ākiṉṟāṉ; cēkkiḻār perumāṉ cīraṭi pōṟṟi, vaḷḷuvar perumāṉ vaḷarpukaḻ pāṭi, civaperumāṉ ceḻuṅkaḻal vāḻvē vāḻvākak koṇṭa perumakaṉ cuppiyā piḷḷi tōṉṟiṉār. tōṉṟiṟ pukaḻoṭu tōṉṟuka! eṉpataṟku ilakkiyamākat tōṉṟiṉār: perumakaṉ, perumāṉ āṉār!
centamiḻum cantaṉamum ticiyellām parimaḷikkap pukaḻ maṇakkum tirunāṭu porunināṭu. annāṭṭiṟku aṇiceyyu tirunakar nelli eṉappeṟum tirunelvēli. ataṟkuk kīḻpāl, peruki vaḷarnta pērūr pāḷiyaṅkōṭṭi. ap pāḷiyaṅkōṭṭiyilē piṟantār tiru.va.cu.
maturiyi aṭuttu vaṭapāl mōkūr eṉṉum paḻimiyāṉatōr ūr uḷatu. avvūrip paḻanāḷil āṭciceytavaṉ paḻiyaṉ māṟaṉ eṉpāṉ. avaṉ iḷiyōṉ iḷam paḻiyaṉ māṟaṉ eṉpāṉ. avaṉ, kōṭṭiyeḻuppik kuṭiyamittuk kōlōcciya iṭam paḻiyaṉ kōṭṭi. ap paḻiyaṉ kōṭṭi piṉṉi nāḷil paḷiyaṉ kōṭṭiyāyp, pāḷiyaṅ kōṭṭiyāyp, pāḷi yāḷ maruviyuḷatu. caṅkappulavar perumakkaḷ pāṭupukaḻukku uriyavaṉāka viḷaṅkiya paḻiyaṉ māṟaṉ ūrilē caṅkanūṟpēḻi taravanta perumakaṉār piṟantatu niṉivukūrat takkatām.
pāḷiyaṅkōṭṭiyil vāḻnta tiruvāḷar oruvar vayira muttup piḷḷi eṉpār. avartam arumik kiḻattiyār cuntarattammiyār. vayiramum, muttum aḻakoṭum viḷaṅkuvati evarē aṟiyār?
oṉṟaṉ kūṟu āṭi uṭuppavar āyiṉum oṉṟiṉār vāḻkkiyē vāḻkki eṉpār oru caṅkaccāṉṟōr. ōr āṭiyi irukūṟu ākki uṭuttukkoṇṭāltāṉ eṉṉa? īruṭalum ōruyirum pōṉṟu vāḻum vāḻvē vāḻvu eṉak kaṇṭa avvuyil kalantu oṉṟiya uyarnta vāḻvi vayiramuttarum cuntarattammiyum koṇṭaṉar. avarkaḷ illaṟavāḻvup payaṉāka makkaḷ aivar tōṉṟiṉar.
mūttavar muttucāmi, aṭuttavar tiruvaraṅkar. mūṉṟāmavar aiyam perumāḷ, nāṉkāmavar ivvaralāṟṟu nāyakar cuppiyā; ivarkaḷukkellām taṅki kuppammāḷ aintāmavar. tiru. va.cu. piṟantatu 1897 ām āṇṭu cepṭampart tiṅkaḷ 22ām nāḷ ākum. tamiḻmoḻi kākka vanta talimakaṉit tam kaṭici makaṉākat tantār cuntarattammiyār. kaṭippaṭṭār talippaṭṭār eṉṉum paḻamoḻi meyyāyiṟṟu. aṉṟiyum tamiḻ aṉṉi ceyta tavapperun tirunāḷum annāḷ āyiṟṟu!


4. iḷamiyum kalviyum
(iḷami, kalvikkuriyatu; āṉāl, av viḷami etta ṉiyō iṭarkaḷic cantikka vēṇṭiyatākavum amintu viṭukiṟatu. av viṭar veḷḷattil etir nīccal aṭikka vallavarkku av viḷami eṉṉa ceykiṉṟatu? eṉṟum iḷamiyāy irukkum pēṟṟi aruḷiviṭukiṟatu.)
makkaḷiṉ kalvi tantiyaric cērntatu eṉpatu muntiyōr muṟi. tantiyoṭu kalvipōm eṉṟu avarkaḷ urattukkūṟiyatum uṇṭu.
vayiramuttuppiḷḷi tam makkaḷukkuk kalvitarum vēṭkiyil munti niṉṟār. muyaṉṟum ceytār. Mdhš mjid K‰WÉ¡f mt® ïUªjhšjhnd Koí«?ōṭum uyiri niṟutti vikka evarāltām muṭikiṉṟatu? vayiramuttupa piḷḷi 1900 ām āṇṭu piṟimuṭip perumāṉ tiruvaṭi yaṭintār.
nalliḷamip paruvattilēyē nāyakaṉi iḻantār cuntarattammiyār. avar eṉṉa ceyvār? tamakku nērnta tīyūḻi eṇṇit terumantu nōvārā? patiṉmūṉṟāṇṭu muttucāmiyiyum, pattāṇṭut tiruvaraṅkariyum, ēḻāṇṭu aiyam perumāḷiyum, mūṉṟāṇṭuc cuppiyāviyum, ōrāṇṭum nirampāta kuppammāḷiyum eṇṇi eṇṇip puṇpaṭuvārā? tampiyar uḷarāka vēṇṭum eṉṉum taṉipperum tayiyāl uyir vāḻnta tilakavatiyār pōlat tam iḷañcelvaṅkaḷiṉ etirkāla vāḻvi nōkki, ampoṉmaṇi nūl tāṅkāmal aruḷ tāṅki niṉṟār.
āliṉ aṭimaram kaṟiyāṉāl arikkappeṟṟu vīḻntālum, maram vīḻntu paṭāmal, ataṉ vīḻtukaḷ kākkum. atupōl tantiyillāk kuṭumpattit tāṅka muṟpaṭṭār mūttavar muttucāmi, avarkkut tantiyār ceytuvanta vaṇikattil ōraḷavu muṉṉarē payiṟciyiruntatu. ap payiṟciyum ārvamum kikoṭuttu utava vaṇikattil īṭupaṭṭār. tiṟamum tērcciyum mikkavarkaḷ ceyyum vaṇikamē tiṭumeṉṟu cāyntuviṭak kāṇkiṟōm. ivviḷiñarāl pōṭṭimikka vaṇika ulakil īṭukoṭukka iyalumā? iyalāmal pōkavē vaṇikam iḻappil muṭintatu.
poruḷ eṉṉum celvac cevili iruntāl kuṭumpam kuṟiviṉṟi naṭakkum. tāṅkuvārum tarippārum palarāka varuvar. talivar illā vīṭu, irunta poruḷum iḻappukku ākiya kuṭumpam; inniliyil eṉṉa ceyya muṭiyum? koṭitu koṭitu vaṟumi koṭitu; ataṉiṉum koṭitu iḷamiyil vaṟumi eṉṟu pulampalām. uṇṭākiya paḷḷatti mēṭākka vaki vēṇṭumē!
paṭippilē pērārvam uṭiya araṅkaṉār tam paṭippi mūṉṟām paṭiva aḷavilē viṭuttu vēli tēṭiṉār. vaḻakkuriñar oruvariṭam eḻuttar vēliyil amarntār. av vēliyil araṅkarkku īṭupāṭu ēṟpaṭavilli. vēṟu vēli tēṭit tūttukkuṭikkuc ceṉṟār. āṅkirunta vaḻutūr aḻakiya cuntarampiḷḷi eṉpār tuṇiyiṉāl kappal ēṟik koḻumpukkuc ceṉṟār. āṅkirunta eḻuttar paṇiyil amarntār. itu nikaḻntatu 1907 ām āṇṭil ākum.
tamiyaṉār iruvarum tam ayarā uḻippāl kuṭumpap poṟiyit tāṅkikkoḷḷavē, iḷiyavar va.cu. pāḷiyiluḷḷa tūya cavēriyār uyarpaḷḷiyil cērntu payiṉṟār. viḷiyum payir muḷiyilē teriyum eṉpataṟku ēṟṟavāṟu kalviyil karuttuṭiyavarāka viḷaṅkiṉār.
periya kuṭumpattit tāy tāṅki niṟkum perumi; ak kuṭumpa varuvāykkākak kalviyi viṭuttum toḻiṟ kaṭamiyilē īṭupaṭṭa mūttavarkaḷ nalluḷḷam; iyalpilē tamakku aminta paṇpāṭu -iviyellām iḷamparuvattilēyē va.cu.vic ciṟup periyār ākkiviṭṭaṉa. ātalāl kālam tavaṟāmi, kaṭamiyil kaṇṇāka iruttal, oḻukkattāl uyartal ākiyavi yellām oruṅkē vaḷara vaḷarntu vantār. itaṉāl āciriyarkaḷ pārāṭṭukku uriyavarāy viḷaṅkiṉār.
āṇṭutōṟum tērvukaḷil cīruṟat tēṟi vakuppil ciṟappiṭam peṟṟār; kaṇakkup pāṭattilum poḻippāṭaṅ kaḷilum iṇiyaṟṟa tiṟam kāṭṭip paricukaḷ peṟṟār.
kalviyil īṭupāṭu kāṭṭum cilar vīṭṭukku utaviyāka iruppatu illi. āṉāl va.cu. aṉṉikku evvaḷavu utaviyāka irukka muṭiyumō avvaḷavu utaviyāka iruntār. vīṭṭup poṟuppukaḷi viruppoṭu ceytār. vītikkuc ceṉṟu veṭṭip poḻutu pōkkuvatō araṭṭiaṭittup poḻuti vīṇākkuvatō iḷam paruvattiliruntē ivar aṟiyātavi.
poḻuti vīṇpaṭuttāta nalla vaḻakkam vāḷā oḻiyumō? poruḷ vakiyilum cikkaṉamāka irukkavum, ciṟu ciṟu tokiyiyum cēmittu vikkavum tūṇṭutalāka amintatu. koḻumpil iruntu arumit tamiyaṉāraṉuppum paṇam ivar peyarukkē varum. vīṭṭukkuc cērkka vēṇṭiya tokiyi vīṭṭukkuc cērppatuṭaṉ, tamakkeṉat taṉiyē varum tokiyiyum kaṇakkiṭṭuc cikkaṉamākac celaviṭṭār.
cikkaṉap paṇpiṟku aṇṇal kāntiyaṭikaḷiyum tanti periyāriyum eṭuttukkāṭṭākak kūṟuvar. avvāṟē ovvoru ciṟuvakiyilum cikkaṉattip pōṟṟiya cemmal
va. cu. āvar. iṉṟiyamiyāt tēvikkē aṉṟip piṟitu etaṟkum celavaḻittalic cālpākak koḷḷāta cāṉṟāṇmi iḷami toṭṭē iruntamiyāla, ivar tam vāḻvil tīyavi eviyum pukāmal taṭuttāṭkoḷḷum tiruppēṟu vāykkap peṟṟār.
iḷamiyilē iccīriya cālpu ciṟantōṅkak kāraṇamāka iruntavi palappala. avaṟṟuḷ taliyāya oṉṟu, ovvoru nāḷum tavaṟāmal varavu celavu eḻutum naṟpaḻakkamākum. poruḷiyal aṟiñarkaḷ valiyuṟuttum im mutal tiṭṭatti mutaṉmiyākak koṇṭu evarum ōtuvikkāmal uṇarntu kaṭippiṭittatu av viḷamparuvattil ceyaṟkarum ceyal eṉpati evarum aṟivar.
kalvit tiṟattilum, paṇpāṭṭilum ciṟantu vaḷarnta
va. cu. 1916 ām āṇṭu paḷḷi iṟutit tērvil veṟṟi peṟṟār. kallūriyil kāl vikka virumpiṉār. āṉāl pollāp poruḷmuṭi vallāṇmi kāṭṭit taṭuttatu. appōtu vāyppāka irunta añcalakap paṇiyil cēra virumpiṉār. āṉāl aṇṇal araṅkaṉār pēruḷḷam vēṟāka iruntatu. tam kalviyippōl tampiyār kalviyum taṭippaṭṭu viṭutal ākātu eṉa eṇṇiṉār. ataṟkut takka tiṭṭamiṭṭār. tampiyik koḻumpukku varumāṟu eḻutiṉār. iḷaval uḷḷamum aṇṇalār eṇṇatti uvantu ēṟṟu āṅkuc cella muntiyatu.
1916 ām āṇṭu cūlit tiṅkaḷ va.cu. koḻumpukkuc ceṉṟār. āṅkirunta mutaṉilik kallūriyil paṭikkat toṭaṅkiṉār. ilaṇṭaṉ mānakara āciriyarkallūri naṭattum tērvu eḻutuvataṟku ak kallūri payiṟci tarum. at tērvu eḻuti veṟṟi peṟṟōr, maruttuvak kallūriyil cērtaṟku vāyppu uṇṭu. ātalāl at tērvu peṟutaṟkup pērārvam koṇṭār va. cu. araṅkaṉār ārvamō tām peṟāp pēṟṟit tampiyarēṉum peṟa vēṇṭum eṉṟa muṉippilē iruntatu. āṉāl, toṭarntu payilvataṟku iyalāmal oru kuṟukkīṭu uṇṭāyiṟṟu.
ilaṅki vāḻvu va.cu. uṭalnilikku ēṟṟuk koḷḷa villi. nīrkkōvi uṇṭāki nīṅkāmal vāṭṭiyatu. ōyāt taṭima nōyuṭaṉ evvaḷavu kālamtāṉ pōrāṭa muṭiyum? muṭiyāmal koḻumpil iruntu pāḷiyaṅkōṭṭikkē tirumpiṉār.
eṭutta paṇikkut taṭiyuṇṭāṉāl palar cōrntu viṭuvar. vāḻvilē ericcalum veṟuppum aṭivar. āṉāl, koḷkiyil uṟutiyāka niṉṟa va. cu. cōrntuviṭavilli. uṟutiyil taḷaravilli. uriya pāṭaṅkaḷi yellām tam ūril iruntu koṇṭē payiṉṟār. muyaṟci meyvaruttak kūli tarāmal pōkātē!
pāḷiyil iruntē payiṉṟa va.cu. tērvukkuriya kālattil mīṇṭum koḻumpukkuc ceṉṟār. tērvit tiṟamāka eḻutiṉār. orē muṟiyil tērcciyum peṟṟār. immuṟi koḻumpukkuc ceṉṟatu ivartam piṟavip payaṉi nilipeṟuttat takka oru vāyppāka amintatu.
araṅkaṉār koḻumpukkuc cellu muṉṉarē tūttukkuṭi aḻakiya cuntarampiḷḷi avarkaḷāl maṟimaliyaṭikaḷārip paṟṟik kēḷvippaṭṭiruntār. ataṉpiṉṉē aṭikaḷār iyaṟṟiya ariya nūlkaḷit taruvittuk kaṟṟu vantār. aṭikaḷāriṉ civattoṇṭukkum, centamiḻttoṇṭukkum tam uḷḷattip paṟikoṭuttu meyyaṭiyārāka viḷaṅkiṉār. aṭikaḷārik koḻumpukku aḻittu avartam vāyuri kēṭkavum peruviruppāyiṉār. ataṉāl aṭikaḷāri āṅku aḻittār.
aṭikaḷār araṅkaṉārik koḻumpilē 1914 ām āṇṭil mutaṟkaṇ kaṇṭār. kaḻipēruvaki yaṭintār. iḷamparuvac cevviyum, vaḷamperukiya uḷḷamum, payiṉ ṟoḻukum paṇpāṭum, tāḷāṇmi mikka vēḷāṇmiyum, tammāṭṭuk koṇṭuḷḷa māṟāta aṉpum aṭikaḷārik kavarntaṉa. viḻā mēṭiyilēyē aṟupattu mūvar nāyaṉmār kaḷi yāṉ aṟivēṉ. āṉāl iṅkē 64 āvatu nāyaṉār uṇmiyi aṟintu aḷavilā makiḻvuṟṟēṉ eṉṟu kūṟi avar ivarē eṉṟu araṅkaṉāri aviyiṉarkku aṟimukap paṭutti viyappil āḻttiṉār. araṅkaṉāri muṉṉarē aṟintiruntavarkaḷ āmām eṉa ēṟṟaṉar. putitāka aṟintōr puṉmuṟuvar pūttu varavēṟṟaṉar. vaḷḷuvarāl pārāṭṭup peṟṟa vaḷarpukaḻ vaṇikac celvaṉpōla, aṭikaḷār pārāṭṭu araṅkaṉārkku vāyttatu.
ivvāṟu arumpi vaḷarnta aṭikaḷār araṅkar toṭarpu piṉṉē mēlum valuppeṟṟatu. aṭikaḷāriṉ nūl veḷiyīṭu viṟpaṉi ākiyavaṟṟukku utaviyatuṭaṉ perumporuḷi naṉkoṭiyākat taṇṭiyum utaviṉār araṅkar. 1917 ām āṇṭu mīṇṭum aṭikaḷārik koḻumpukku aḻittār. appoḻutu iḷaval va.cu. koḻumpukkut tērvu eḻutu vataṟkākac ceṉṟirunta kālam. ātalāl, aṭikaḷār toṭarpu ivarukku vāyppatāyiṟṟu.
aṭikaḷār kulacēkarapaṭṭiṉam centilāṟumukampiḷḷi avarkaḷ vaḷamaṉiyil taṅkiyiruntār. avarōṭu uṭaṉuṟintu uriyāṭi makiḻum pēṟu ivarkku uṇṭāyatu. piṉṉāḷil yāḻnūl iyaṟṟiya perumpēriciyāciriyar vipulāṉanta aṭikaḷār annāḷil mayilvākaṉar eṉṉum peyaruṭaṉ kallūri māṇavarāka viḷaṅkiṉār. avaraṭikaḷār uṟiyum vaḷamaṉikku vantu pintamiḻ moḻi kuṟittum, centamiḻc civaneṟi kuṟittum ārvamuṟak kēṭṭum, aiyuṟṟaṉa viṉāvit teḷintum celvār. appoḻutellām uṭaṉiruntu iṉpam māntum perumpēṟu ivarkku vāyttatu. av vāyppāl aṭikaḷārmēlkoṇṭa paṟṟup perukip peruki muḻumatiyāka innāḷ viḷaṅkutal kaṇkūṭu.
ilaṇṭaṉ mānakarat tērviṉ tērcci muṭivu vantatu. atil veṟṟi peṟṟār va.cu. aṭutta muyaṟci eṉṉa? ceṉṉi maruttuvak kallūriyilcērntu payila muyaṟci mēṟkoṇṭār. 1918 ām āṇṭu cūṉ tiṅkaḷ pallāvarattiṟkuc ceṉṟu aṭikaḷār vaḷamaṉiyil taṅkikkoṇṭu ceṉṉi maruttuvak kallūriyil cēra muyaṉṟār. am muyaṟci veṟṟitarāmiyāl mīṇṭum pāḷiyaṅkōṭṭikkut tirumpiṉār.
paṭippārvam iṭiyīṭu iṉṟi untiyāmiyāl tirunelvēli intuk kallūriyil iṭinili vakuppil cērntu, iraṇṭāṇṭukaḷ payiṉṟār. avvaḷavil kallūrik kalvi muṟṟuppeṟṟatu. āṉāl tāmē kaṟkum kalvikkuk kāla elli uṇṭō? aṟitōṟu aṟiyāmi kaṇṭaṟṟu eṉṉum vaḷḷuvarvākkukku ēṟpattuṟitōṟum puttaṟivip perukkik koḷḷalāṉār.


5. epporuḷum āṉa tiruvaraṅkar
(uṭaṉpiṟantē kollum nōy; uṭaṉpiṟantār cuṟṟattār eṉṟirukka vēṇṭā eṉṟu tērnturittatum inta maṇtāṉ. tampiyi iṉṟik kiṭappaṉō tamiyaṉ maṇmēl eṉṟatum inta maṇtāṉ. taṉittaṉi nāṭi pārttu muṭivu ceyyum tamiḻ maruttuva muṟippaṭi muṭivu ceyya vēṇṭiyatu itu.)
tēṉamarum cōlit tiruvaraṅkar epporuḷum āṉavar kāṇ ammāṉi eṉat tiruvaraṅkak kalampakam pāṭum tiruvaraṅkar, epporuḷum āṉavar eṉpati ivarkkum kūṟattakum.
caṟṟu vaḻukkiyuṭaṉ kūṭiya kaṉatta periya tali, nīṟu viḷaṅkum paranta neṟṟi; periya kaṇkaḷ; iṉikka iṉikkap pēcum kuṟunakimukam; akaṉṟa mārpu; veḷḷi uṭcaṭṭi; ivikaḷ ellāvaṟṟilum palavakikkaṭitak kaṭṭukaḷ uṭiyatum, pottāṉ pōṭappaṭātutum ākiya mēṟcaṭṭi; iṭuppiṟ cuṟṟiyuḷḷa cempaṭṭu, kaṇukkālukku mēṟpaṭṭa veḷḷi vēṭṭi; aintaṭi uyaram; nimirntu etirnōkki naṭakkum vīranaṭi - ivi tiruvaraṅkariṉ ōviyam.
tiruvaraṅkar ārvam aṭikaḷārik koḻumpukku aḻittatōṭu niṉṟatō? avarkku aṇukkarāki niṟkum aḷavil amintatō? illi! illi! avar nūlkaḷit taruvittu viṟkavum, avar iyaṟṟiya nūlkaḷi veḷiyiṭutaṟku ākum vakiyil poruḷ taṇṭi viṭukkavum āka vaḷarntatu aṉṟō! ammaṭṭō? aṭikaḷāriṉ nūlkaḷi viṟṟu utavutaṟ keṉṟē oru viṟpaṉi niliyattik koḻumpil toṭaṅkavum av vuḷḷaṉpu tūṇṭiyatu. aṉpu eṉṉatāṉ ceyyātu?
araṅkaṉāriṉ keḻutaki naṇparvi. caṅkaranārāyaṇa piḷḷi eṉpār. tam peyariyum avar peyariyum iṇittuk tirucaṅkar kampeṉi (tiruvaraṅkar caṅkaranārāyaṇar kampeṉi) eṉṉum niṟuvaṉatti 1917 il niṟuviṉār. ataṉ kiḷi niliyatti 1918 il ceṉṉiyilum tōṟṟuvittār. in niṟuvaṉamē civacittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakam tōṉṟutaṟku vāytta muṉṉōṭiyāka amintatu. aṉṟiyum aṭikaḷāriṉ nūlkaḷ cīriya vakiyil veḷippaṭṭuc centamiḻar celvamāka viḷaṅkutaṟku vakiyum ceytatu. aṭikaḷāriṉ ārāyccik karuvūlamāṉa tiruvācaka virivuri in niṟuvaṉattiṉ vaḻiyākavē centamiḻk kaḷañciyam eṉṉum peyaril mutaṟkaṇ veḷip paṭṭatu eṉpatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu.
tiruvaraṅkar iyalpilēyē kūrnta mūḷiyar; māntar iyalpi ūṭuruvi nōkka valla āṟṟal paṭittavar; muyaṟci, cuṟucuṟuppu, kaṭippiṭi, iṉimi,. oppuravu mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ taḻumpēṟiyavar; perumakkaḷiṭam naṭpāṭum pāṅkum avar kaḷiṭam paṇikoḷḷum pāṉmiyum kivarap peṟṟavar. ep paṇiyiyum arumiyuṭittu eṉṟu ayartal aṟiyātavar; kaṇkāṇippiṟ kaṇṭippum, pakiyil viḻippum, paḻamiyil kaṇṇōṭṭamum kaviṉap peṟṟavar.
kaṭitam varitalil kālam tavaṟāk karuttuṇarvāḷar. avari av vakiyil veṟṟi koḷvār ariyar; āṅkilam aruntamiḻ ākiya irumoḻikaḷilum tiruttamāka eḻutum tiṟalar; eḻutuma kaṭitaṅkaḷil paḻantamiḻ nūl mēṟkōḷkaḷi ūṭāṭa viṭṭu uṇarcciyūṭṭa valvar.
tokuppuk kaliccelvar avar. caṭṭip pikaḷ puṭittu niṟkat tokuppuk kuṟippukaḷ, kaṭitak kaṟṟikaḷ, aṭittuk koṇṭu irukkum. paḻañcuvaṭikaḷ, itaḻkaḷ, tiṅkaḷ muttiṅkaḷ āṇṭu veḷiyīṭukaḷ ākiyavaṟṟit tokuttum, tokuttavaṟṟi vaṉappuṟa vakuttum, vakuttavaṟṟi vaḷamuṟap pēṇikkāttum avar tāmē eṇṇi eṇṇi iṭiyīṭu iṉṟic ceyta ceyaṟkarum ceyalkaḷ niṉitōṟum iṉpam payappaṉavām. iv viyaṟkit tiṟaṉālivarcērttu vitta aruṅkalic celvaṅkaḷ iṉṟiya nōkkil matippil aṭaṅkutaṟku uriyaṉa alla. maṟṟoṉṟu ivartam arumperun tiṟalkaḷ ellām viñcip perukkeṭuttuk kuṉṟiṉmēl iṭṭa viḷakkāki nāṭṭukku naṟpayaṉ viḷikka oru tampi va. cu. vi uruvākkiyatu.
an nāḷil araṅkar ceyta vaḻikāṭṭutal - avar ōtātu kaṟṟavaṟṟi uṭaṉiruntu ōti ōti iḷaval va. cu. uṇarnta pēṟṟāl, nāṭu peṟṟa nalam ēṭumaṇakka eḻutat takkatām. ātalāl tāṉ aṇṇaṉum tampiyumākiya ivarkaḷi neṭuṅkālam aṟintu nēril paḻaki aṟintavarkaḷ tamatu toṇṭu viḷaṅkutaṟkāṉa tampiyip peṟṟa pēṟu ivarukkē vāyttatu eṉṟu uḷamārap pārāṭṭuvārāyiṉar. empiyi īṅkup peṟṟēṉ eṉṉeṉakkariya teṉṟāṉ eṉṉum vākkik kaṭṭik kākkap piṟanta uṭaṉpiṟantār araṅkarum 
va. cu. vum eṉpatil aiyamilliyām.


6. nūṟpatippuk kaḻakapperumi
(etirē celkiṟārē avar kōṭi kōṭiyākat tēṭi vittuḷ celvar. āṉāl, nāṉ maṉappāṭam ceytuḷḷa ilakkiyac celvattiṟku iṇiyākumā avar celvam?
pullik kāṭṭikkoṇṭu pōkiṉṟavaṉit toṭarum āvippōl, nūlit tokuttu vittiruppāṉit tēṭi nāṉ pōkiṉṟēṉ).
- cuviñar iruvar coṉmoḻi.
poruḷ illārkku iv vulakam illi - uṇmi tāṉ. poruḷallavarip poruḷākac ceyyum, poruḷalla tilli poruḷ - ituvum uṇmitāṉ. poruḷiṉāl ākum ellām eṉpataṟkāka ap poruḷi eppaṭiyum tēṭuvatu takumā? takkavarkaḷukkut takumā?
poruḷ tēṭutaṟkuriya vāyilkaḷuḷ taliyāyatu vāṇikam. āṉāl, ellā vāṇikaṅkaḷum ceyyattakkaṉavē eṉiṉum cāṉṟōr uḷḷam avaṟṟil īṭupaṭuvatu illi. mācu maṟuvaṟṟa perumi taruvatuṭaṉ matikkattakka payaṉiyum nāṭṭukkut taruvatākat tam vāṇikak kaṭami iruppatu nalameṉ aṉṉōr eṇṇi īṭupaṭuvar. poruḷ oṉṟē avarkaḷ poruṭṭāka eṇṇi īṭupaṭāta vaṇikaneṟi atu.
koḷḷiyō koḷḷi eṉṟu kūṟumāṟu ettaṉi ettaṉi vaṇikap pakaṟkoḷḷikaḷ, ettaṉiettaṉi kalappaṭaṅkaḷ, kiyūṭṭukaḷ, kaḷḷac cantikaḷ, kaṭattalkaḷ, patukkalkaḷ iviyellām oru ciṟitum illāta vaṇikamum uṇṭō? uṇṭu eṉpatum uṇmiyē!
caṅkaccāṉṟōr kūṟiyatupōlak koḷvatum miki koḷātu, koṭuppatum kuṟikoṭāta aṟavili vāṇikamē oruvar puriyiṉum, avar eṭuttukkoṇṭa vāṇikattip poṟuttu avartam uḷappāṉmi veḷippaṭumām. uyir kākkum uṇavuvaki, māṉaṅkākkum uṭivaki, uṟṟavakiyil ellām utavum valavakip payaṉ poruḷkaḷ, palacarakku, aḻakup poruḷkaḷ ākiya vaṇikaṅkaḷ ellām ulakiyal naṭimuṟikku vēṇṭuvaṉavē; mēṟkoḷḷat  takkaṉavē! ivvāṇikaṅkaḷil nērmi kāṇappeṟiṉ arumiyām. aḵtu atil īṭupaṭṭuḷḷa naṉmakkaḷ uḷḷattu ūṟṟeṭutta peruṅkuṇattiṉ veṟṟiyāl amintatē eṉalām.
 āṉāl, ivvellā vāṇikaṅkaḷiṉum taliyāyatu oṉṟu uṇṭu eṉiṉ, aḵtu aṟivu nūl vāṇikamē eṉat tuṇintu kūṟalām.
marattiṉ vaḷivi nērākkavā, kalli nērāy uṭikkavā, maṉiyiṭatti nērāy aṟukkavā, nūlaṭittē ceyyapaṭum. atupōl maṉattiluḷḷa kōṇalkaḷi akaṟṟi nērākka vallatu aṟivu nūl. avvaṟivu nūlkaḷi veḷiyiṭṭu, viṟpaṉi ceyyum vaṇikatti mēṟkoḷvatu kaṇṇoḷi illārkkuk kaṇṇoḷi vaḻaṅkum vaḷḷaṉmi pōlvatām! aṟaṅkaḷ eṉac collappaṭum, ellāvaṟṟiyum ceyyum arumiyilum av vaṟattiṟku nilikkaḷamāka niṉṟu eṉṟum kākkum nūlkaḷi veḷiyiṭutal miku perumikkuriyatēyām.
nūlkaḷ veḷiyīṭu eṉpatilum ettakiya puṉmikaḷ palkiyuḷa? iḷiñar vāḻvik keṭuttu eṉṟum kaṭittēṟa oṭṭā iḻivil taḷḷum kīḻnili nūlkaḷ ettaṉi? kiyāl toṭṭālum kaṇṇāl kaṇṭālum cavarkkāram iṭṭuk kaḻuviṉālum tūymiyākāta kayami nūlkaḷ ettaṉi? piṟanta mēṉip paṭaṅkaḷi veḷiyiṭṭup pētiyar uḷḷatti vayappaṭutti aḻikkum iḻitaki nūlkaḷ ettaṉi? ivaṟṟi eḻutuvittum patippittum vāṇikam ceyvati nūl vaṇikam eṉṟu collalum iḻivēyām. ivi ettīya vaṇikattiṉum tīya vaṇikamām. ēṉeṉil maṟṟit tīya vaṇikaṅkaḷ vaḻivaḻi uṭaliyum poruḷiyum maṭṭumē keṭukkum. ittīya vaṇikamō, uyirppiyē oṭukkuvatu! uyirppi oṭukkiya piṉṉē uyvētum uṇṭō? yātumilliyatām!
aṟivunūl vāṇikattil talippaṭuvārkkut taṉit takutikaḷ vēṇṭum piṟa vāṇikaṅkaḷukkup poruḷum muyaṟciyum iruppiṉ veṟṟiyām. āṉāl aṟivu nūl vāṇikam ceytaṟku aṟivuk kūrppum, aṟavuṇarvum, aruṭperukkum iṉṟiyamiyātu vēṇṭattakkavi. oru paḷḷi āciriyaṉ ceyyum piḻi ap paḷḷiyaḷavil kēṭu ceytu ceytu amiyum. āṉāl, oru nūlāciriyaṉ ceyyum piḻi nāṭṭiyē nalivikkum eṉṉum peru neñcam vāynta perumakkaḷē ittuṟiyil talippaṭat takkavarkaḷ. ivvakiyāl nōkkiṉ oru palkalikkaḻakap paṇiyiṉum mēmpaṭṭa paṇiyeṉṟu collattakkatu aṟivunūl patippakappaṇi eṉṟu malimēl ēṟiniṉṟu talinimirntu kūṟuttakka taṉipperumiyuṭiyatām.
puḻuvāyp pūcciyāy vīḻntu maṭiya iruppāri, iruciṟaku, eḻuppa eḻumpum paṟavipōla uyarattiṉ uyarattil paṟakka viṭuvatu etuvō, nilipeṟā vāḻvi nilipeṟutta vallatu etuvō, nampikki iḻantupōy naliyum vāḻvi, nampikkiyūṭṭit taṭṭi niṟutti nallapala ceytaṟku ēvuvatu etuvō, toyvillāt tuṇivum, eḻucciyum toṭarntu taravallatu etuvō, mācukaḷi akaṟṟi maṇiyāka oḷiviṭac ceyya vallatu etuvō, kōṭṭatti nimirtti nērākka vallatu etuvō. koḷkiyil piṟaḻāk koḻumiyik koṇṭoḻuka vippatu etuvō, ivaṉ cāṉṟōṉ eṉṟu pallāyiravar iṭiyē iruntālum oruvaṉi iṉaṅkaṇṭu talivaṇaṅkac ceyyum takavaḷippatu etuvō atuvē aṟivu nūl ātaliṉ, attaku nūlkaḷi veḷiyiṭum niṟuvaṉat toṇṭukku iṇi atuvēyeṉṟu kūṟuvatu aṉṟivēṟoṉṟu kūṟutaṟku illiyām.
iṉṉum aṟivunūl veḷiyīṭē aṟappaṇiyāy irukka, appaṇiyāl aṭiyavarum ūtiyattil cempātiyi aṟavaḻi kaḷukkē celaviṭa vēṇṭumeṉa varampukaṭṭi vāykkāl kōli aṟappayiri vaḷarttu varutaṟkut tiṭṭam tīṭṭiyatum, ataṉic cikkeṉak kaṭippiṭittuc cīriya aṟaniṟuvaṉaṅkaḷ palavaṟṟit tōṟṟuvittuk kaṇṇiṉ maṇiyāyk kāttu varuvatum, poṉ malarukkup putumaṇamum vāyttatu pōṉṟa perumikku uriyatām. ivaṟṟiyellām eṇṇi uṇarvōr, iṟṟikku aṟupatu yāṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉṉē civacittāntak kaḻakakkūṭṭup paṅku niṟuvaṉam tōṟṟuvitta arumiyi niṉintu viyantu pōṟṟāmal irār. kūṭṭuṟavu amippu muṟiyilum, puttaka veḷiyīṭṭu niṟuvaṉat taṉmiyilum ic civacittāntakkaḻakam taṉṉikaraṟṟa taṉic ciṟappāl, ulakukku oru māmaṇiyāy viḷaṅkutali ivvaralāṟṟiṉkaṇ āṅkāṅku aṟintu makiḻka.


7. kaḻakak kālkōḷ
(ōṭatti ōṭṭutaṟkākat tuṭuppu valikka muṉintāṉ avaṉ. āṉāl, oru kappaṟkāraṉ tāṉē avaṉit tēṭivantu ak kappalukku mīkāmaṉ ākki viṭṭāṉ.)
nūṟpatippuk kaḻakattiṉ ciṟappi aṟintōm. nūṟpatippuk kaḻakac ciṟappu eṉpatu eṉṉa? ataṉit tōṟṟuvittuk kaṭṭik kāppavar ciṟappēyaṉṟō!
koḻumpilē tōṉṟiya tirucaṅkar kampeṉi ōrāṇṭē āṅku naṭipeṟṟatu. an niṟuvaṉattiṉ mēl araṅkarkku uṇṭākiya paṟṟum, toḻil tiṟamum ataṉiyē tam vāḻvuk kaṭamiyākak koḷḷa ēviyatu. ātalāl ataṉit tāyt tamiḻakattilē niṟuvip paṇiceyvatē muṟimiyum kaṭamiyumām eṉa muṭivu ceytār. muṭivu oṉṟu eṭuttuviṭṭāl uṭaṉē atil muḻumūccāka iṟaṅkip paṇiceyvatu avar taṉmi. ātalāl koḻumpil iruntu pāḷikku vantār. āṅkiruntu ceṉṉikkuc ceṉṟār. aṭikaḷāruṭaṉ kalantu uriyāṭiṉār. piṉṉarc ceṉṉi, pavaḻakkāratteru 2 āmeṇ (putu eṇ 87) kaṭṭiṭattil irunta teruppakkattu aṟiyil tirucaṅkar kampeṉiyit toṭaṅkiṉār.
tirucaṅkar kampeṉiyic ceṉṉiyil toṭaṅkiya aṉṟē pāḷiyaṅkōṭṭiyil uḷḷa tam illattil ataṉ kiḷi niliyam toṭaṅkappeṟṟatu. araṅkar ceṉṉi niliyap poṟuppip pārttuk koṇṭār. iḷaval va.cu. kiḷiniliyap poṟuppi ēṟṟuk koṇṭār. tamiḻakattiṉ vaṭa elliyilum, teṉ elliyilum tirucaṅkar kampeṉi orēvēḷiyilirukiḷikaḷi virittatu. atu tamiḻ ulaka muḻuvatum taṇṇiḻal parapputaṟkuriya oru peruvāyppu uṇṭātaṟkut tūṇṭukōlāyiṟṟu. nāḷ ceyvati nallavar ceyyār eṉpatu paḻamoḻi. nāḷum nallavarum cērntu ceytālnaṭavātaṉavum naṭakkum; nikaḻātaṉavum nikaḻum!
aṭikaḷārkku aṉparāy nelliyilē tiru. mā. tiraviyam piḷḷi eṉpār oruvar tikaḻntār. avar aṭikaḷārip paṉmuṟi nellikku aḻippittuc coṉ maḻi poḻiyac ceytār. aṭikaḷāriṉ aṟivup payir vaḷarccikku avar tiraviyamākavum iruntār. avarkku aṉpar tiru. ce. vicuvanāta piḷḷi eṉpār. avar tamiḻum civamum taḻittōṅka vēṇṭum eṉpatil tīrākkātalar. nammakkaḷ ettaṉiyō putupputut toḻilmuṟikaḷil īṭupaṭukiṉṟaṉar. uyirkku ūtiyamām. ippaṇiyil īṭupaṭuvār ,larē eṉṉum eṇṇam uṭiyavarāka iruntār. tam karuttit tiruviyam piḷḷiyiṉiṭam veḷiyiṭṭār. tiraviyampiḷḷiaṭikaḷār tuṇiyi avāviṉār. aṭikaḷāreṇṇam araṅkarmēl paṭintatu.
itaṉi itaṉāl ivaṉmuṭikkum eṉṟāyntu
ataṉi avaṉkaṇ viṭal.
eṉpataṟku ēṟpa aṭikaḷār araṅkari aṇukumāṟu āṟṟuppaṭuttiṉār. aṭikaḷārvaḻivantaavvāṟṟuppaṭi aruntamiḻ araṅkarkku aḷappilā āṉantattēṉāka amintatu.
tiraviyampiḷḷi, vaḻakkuriñar oruvariṭam eḻutta
rākap paṇi ceytu vantavar. teṉṉintiya vaṅki vitikaḷit tiṟamāka amittut tantavar avar. ākaliṉ kaḻaka vitikaḷi arumiyāy amitr. appoḻutu uṭaṉiruntu utaviṉār iḷavalār va. cu. cīriya māḷiki eḻupputaṟku muṉṉē variyum varipaṭattil aṉṟō ataṉ amippum aḻakum arumiyum vaḷarcci vāyppum ellām aṭaṅkik kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa.āliṉ vittil ap perumarap pakutiyellām aṭaṅkik kiṭattal pōla, amippu vitikaḷilētām aṭaṅkik kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa, oruniṟuvaṉattiṉ ceyaṉmuṟikaḷ eṉpatu uṇmiyām. itaṉi etirkāla vaḷarccik kaṇṇōṭṭattōṭu vakutta mikkat tuṇi niṉṟār, va. cu. eṉpatu avviḷamikkē perumi taruvatām. tiṭṭamiṭṭut terintaminta kaḻaka vitimuṟi kaḷi vaḻakkuriñar tiru. ti. kōmati nāyakampiḷḷi avarkaḷ caripārttu utaviṉār.
kaḻakattiṉ mutaṟporuḷ urū. 50,000 eṉṟum, atu paṅku oṉṟukku urū. 10 viḻukkāṭu 5000 paṅkukaḷākak koṭukkappaṭum eṉṟum, tiruvaraṅkaṉārum tiraviyampiḷḷiyum kūṭṭamiccar
kaḷāka iruntu kaḻakatti naṭattuvatu eṉṟum, kaḻakam pativu ceyyap peṟṟa kālam mutal irupattintu āṇṭukaḷ avarkaḷum avarkaḷ vaḻittōṉṟalkaḷum amiccar
kaḷākavum kaḻakac ceyal kuḻu uṟuppiṉarkaḷākavum viḷaṅkik kaḻakatti naṭāttum urimi uṭiyavar eṉṟum uṟutimuṟi eḻutappeṟṟatu. piṉ tirunelvēlit teṉṉintiya civacittānta nūṟpatippukkaḻakam limiṭeṭ eṉum peyarāl 21-9-1920 il tirunelvēliyil kaḻakam pativu ceyyappeṟṟatu. tiru. va.cu. piṟanta cepṭampar 22ām nāḷ. kaḻakampativu ceyyap peṟṟa nāḷ cepṭampar 21 ām nāḷ. in nāḷnerukkam, ivarkkum kaḻakattiṟkum uḷḷa nerukkattik kāṭṭum mutaṟkuṟippup pōlum.
kaḻakam pativāṉatum araṅkaṉār tirunelvēliyilēyē taṅkutaṟku nērntatu. ataṉālavar ceṉṉiyil naṭattivanta tirucaṅkar kampeṉi puttaka vaṇikattit toṭarntu naṭattum poṟuppiṉi iḷavalāriṭam oppaṭittār. 1921 pipravarit tiṅkaḷil tirucaṅkarkampeṉi irunta pavaḻakkāratteru iraṇṭām eṇ aṟiyilēyē kaḻakak kiḷiniliyam toṭaṅkappeṟṟatu. ataṉ mukavarāka va. cu. amarntār. atu mutal puttakavaṇikam kaḻakattiṉ peyarālalēyē naṭi
peṟalāyiṟṟu! tirucaṅkar kampeṉi eṉṉum ciṟṟāṟu, kaḻakap pērāṟṟuṭaṉ kalantu oṉṟāki amintatu.
kaḻakattiṟkut tiraviyampiḷḷi vaḻiyāka vittiṭṭavar tiru. vicuvanātapiḷḷi allarō! avar pattu urūpā peṟumāṉa nūṟu paṅkukaḷi uvappuṭaṉ ēṟṟukkoṇṭār. tiṭṭappaṭi 5000 paṅkukaḷ allavō vēṇṭum? amiccarkaḷ iruvarum paṅkukaḷ cērppatil muṉintaṉar. tiraviyampiḷḷi pala tiṟappaṇikaḷil īṭupaṭṭavar. vāytta poḻutukiḷil maṭṭum kaḻakappaṇikaḷil karuttuk koḷvār. araṅkarō āviyum uṭalum uṭimi ellāmum kaḻakattoṇṭukkē āḷākkip paṇiceytār. kaḻakam kālkoṇṭatu.
kaḻakam kāl koṇṭu kaviṉuṟat tulaṅkuvati aṟintār tamiḻ nūl tokuppārvalar pākaṉēri mu. kācivicuvanātaṉ ceṭṭiyār. kaḻakat tiṭṭaṅkaḷum, kaṇṇikkavarum ataṉ patippukaḷum avarik kavarntaṉa. taṉaviciya iḷiñar tamiḻccaṅkat talivarākavum, puravalarākavum, tikaḻum avar kaḻakap puravalarākavum amiya ārvam koṇṭār. pattu urūpā peṟumāṉa nūṟu paṅkukaḷi avar ēṟṟukkoṇṭār. itaṉāl, kaḻakattoṭakkattil nūṟu paṅku eṭuttukkoṇṭa vicuvanātar kaḻakattiṟku vittiṭṭavar eṉṟāl, iv vicuvanātar kaḻakappayir ceḻittu naṟpayaṉ vaḻaṅka vēliyiṭṭa vittakarāvar eṉac cāṉṟōrkaḷāl pukaḻum pēṟu peṟṟār. iruvarum vicuvanātap pēruṭiyār eṉpatu karutattakum ciṟappaṉṟō!


8. kaḻaka nōkkac ciṟappu
(taṉakkākavē piṟar vāḻavēṇṭum eṉpatu āṇavanōkku, tāṉē vāḻavēṇṭum eṉpatu mika mikak kuṟukiya nōkku, tāṉum vāḻavēṇṭum piṟarum vāḻavēṇṭum eṉpatu potu nōkku; taṉakkākap piṟarum, piṟarukkākat tāṉum vāḻa vēṇṭum eṉpatu oppunōkku; piṟarukkākavē tāṉvāḻa vēṇṭum eṉpatu perunōkku. in nōkkam uṭiyavarāl tāṉ ulakam uṇkiṟatu.)
nōkku eṉpatu paḻantamiḻc col. ceyyuḷiyal kūṟavanta āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār ataṉ uṟuppukaḷik kūṟuṅkāl nōkku eṉpatoṉṟiyum kuṟippiṭukiṉṟār. ataṉ ilakkaṇam iṉṉateṉavum teḷivikkiṟār. muḻutuṟa nōkkik kaḻi pēriṉpam koḷvatē nōkku eṉpatu avar karutum poruḷākum.
pārvi parantupaṭṭuc celvatu; kāṭci kaṇṇukkuk kaḷippūṭṭuvatu; nōkku uḷḷattiṟku uvakiyūṭṭuvatu.
pārvi kaṇkaḷi iyalpāy akalat tiṟakkap piṟantu eṇum poruḷilum paravic cellum.
kāṭci kaṇṇi ārvattāl tiṟappittu uṇarvi uṇṭākkip pārāṭṭa vikkum.
nōkkō, kuvintu ceṉṟu uḷḷattiṉ āḻattil patintu uṇarvi uṇṭākki meymmaṟakka vikkum. in nōkki nōkkutaṟ kāraṇam nōkkeṉap paṭumē eṉa nayamikak kuṟittār tolkāppiyaṉār. kavikaṇ nōkku eṉa nōkkiṉi ōviyamākkik kāṭṭiṉār oru caṅkac cāṉṟōr. nōkku, kātal poruṭṭātali vaḷḷuvar mutalākac colliyal uṇarnta pulavarkaḷ ellārum cuvikeḻumap pāṭiṉar.
nōkkil iruntu kiḷarntatu nōkkam. nōkkam ārvamikka kaṭippiṭiyāyiṟṟu. atuvē kuṟikkōḷ āyiṟṟu. kōḷ eṉpatu eṉṉa? koḷḷuki, kaṭippiṭi eṉka. māntar vāḻvu, nērkkoṭu paṭṭatu. illiyeṉil, pōkkoṭu pōyk keṭṭoḻintatēyām. ittakiya nōkkiṉ nuṭpamum, payaṉum, iṉṟiyamiyāmiyum uṇarntu amikkappaṭṭatē kaḻaka nōkkam ākum.
taṉiyāṭu mēyppatiṉum kūṭṭāṭu naṉṟām;
	iṉināṭē kūṭṭu ṟavi nāṭu
eṉpatu kūṭṭuṟaviṉ ciṟappu valiyuṟuttal. kūṭṭāṭu mēyttal nāṭṭup puṟaṅkaḷil kāṇakkūṭiyatē. kālvaluvāka ūṉṟavu māṭṭāta - ūṉṟutali eṇṇavum māṭṭāta - kālattē, nūṟpatippu niṟuvaṉattil pukuttiyatē cīriya nōkkākum. in nōkkil tuvaṅkic ciṟanta paṇiceytuvarum potuk kaḻakam teṉṉāṭṭil iḵtu oṉṟēyākum.
kaḻaka ūtiyattil iruntu nūṟṟukku āṟu viḻukkāṭṭukku mēl paṅkāḷikaḷ ūtiyam peṟutaṟku urimi illi eṉpatu aṟantaḻuviya - poruḷē mutaṉmiyeṉak karutāta - oru nōkkam ākum.
maṟṟoṉṟu, kaḻakattukku ēṟpaṭum celavu nīkkik kiṭikkum ūtiyattil caripāti ik kaḻakattiṉ āṭciyil uḷḷa teṉṉintiya civacittānta caṅkattiṟkum, teṉṉintiya tamiḻc caṅkattiṟkum koṭukkap peṟutal vēṇṭum eṉpatu. it tērcci mikka tiṭṭattāl ic caṅkaṅkaḷ civa ulakukkum tamiḻ ulakukkum ceytuḷḷa - ceytuvarum - ciṟanta toṇṭukaḷ matippiṭaṟku ariyaṉa.
iṉṉum, entavoru veḷiyīṭṭakamum itukāṟum mēṟkoḷḷappeṟāta nikaraṟṟa iraṇṭu tiṭṭaṅkaḷi vakuttuk koṇṭatu kaḻakam.
oṉṟu : ārāycciyāḷarkku aruntuṇi yākattakka vakiyil kiṭittaṟku ariya nūlkaḷiyellām tokuttu nūlniliyam ākkutal eṉpatu. it tiṭṭattāl ivvulakiṉ enta oru nūl niliyattilum tiṭṭavaṭṭamākap peṟutaṟku muṭiyavē muṭiyātu eṉattakka pallāyiram aruntamiḻ nūlkaḷi annāḷ mutalē tokuttu vikkum pēṟu vāyttatu. iḵtu ulakameṅkum uḷḷa enta veḷiyīṭṭu niṟuvaṉamum ceytaṟiyāta ceyalākum.
maṟṟoṉṟu: itaḻ veḷiyiṭutal eṉṉum nōkkam. in nōkkattāl aruṅkalic celvameṉa matittu aṭṭi kaṭṭi, vittup pēḻiyuḷ eṉṟum pōṟṟik kākkattakka karuvūlamākac centamiḻc celvi eṉṉum tiṅkaḷitaḻ taṭippaṭutal illāmal 1923 ām āṇṭut toṭakka mutalē arumperun toṇṭāṟṟi varukiṟatu.
iviyellām kaḻaka nōkkac ciṟappiṉ aṭippaṭi
yāl nāṭu peṟṟa naṟpayaṉkaḷām.
iṉi, nūlkaḷilum ennūl āyiṉum veḷiyiṭṭu viṭuvatā? illi! avaṟṟiyum muṉṉatākavē aṟutiyiṭṭu uṟutip paṭuttap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
civacittānta nūlkaḷiyum avaṟṟiṉ karuttukku māṟupāṭillāta paḻiya aṭiṟavu nūlkaḷ, karuvi nūlkaḷ, ulaka nūlkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiyum ituvari accukku varāta viyum, ippōtu kiṭittaṟku ariyavāyuḷḷaṉavumāṉa nūlkaḷiyum patippittu viṟpaṉi ceytal,
mēṟkuṟitta nūlkaḷukku aṟivuṭiya periyār palarik koṇṭu ārāyccikaḷ, viḷakkavurikaḷ mutaliyaṉa eḻutuvittu avaṟṟip patippittu viṟpaṉi ceytal.
vaṭamoḻiyilum maṟṟum piṟamoḻikaḷilumuḷḷa aṟiya nūlkaḷit tamiḻil moḻipeyarttup patippittal, viṟṟu mutal ceytal.
tamiḻ nūlkaḷi āṅkilattilum piṟamoḻikaḷilum moḻi peyarttup patippittu viṟṟumutal ceytal.
- iviyellām ettakiya etirkāla nōkkoṭu amikkap peṟṟavi eṉpatik karuti makiḻka.
iṉṉoṉṟu : tamiḻmoḻi innāḷil evvet tuṟi kaḷil peṟṟirukkum ēṟṟaṅkaḷukkum aṭippaṭiyāka aminta kaḻakattiṉ tiṭṭamiṭṭa paṇikaḷēyām.. taṉittamiḻ iyakkap pācaṟiyākat tikaḻntu varuvarum kaḻakamēyām. ivaṟṟuk kellām kāraṇarāka iruntavar evar? araṅkaṉārum, avar arumi iḷaval va. cu. vumē āvar aṉṟō!
kaḻakam eṉṉum col paḻaṅkālattil perumip poruḷ taruvatāy illi. tivākaramuṉivar, parañcōti muṉivar ākiyōr kālattukkup piṉṉē tāṉ kaḻakam eṉpatu cāṉṟōr aviyam eṉṉum poruṭciṟappuṭiya collāyiṟṟu. ac colli nāṭaṟinta perumic collāka māṟṟi vaḻaṅku vittatu ic civa cittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakamēyām. ik kaḻakam tōṉṟic cīriya paṇikaḷ ceytu kaḻakac collukkut taṉip perumi cērtta piṉṉarē, kaṭcikaḷum amippukaḷum aviyaṅkaḷum kaḻakap peyar koṇṭaṉa. iviyellām eṇṇi makiḻattakkavi eṉka.


9. āṭciyiṉ arumi
(ēr uḻutaliṉum nalamākum eruviṭutal; eru viṭutaliṉum nalamākum kaḷiyeṭuttal; kaḷiyeṭuttaliṉum nalamākum nīrppāyccutal; nīrppāyccutaliṉum nalamākum kāval purital; kāval cōrntālkippiṭi tavacamēṉum kaḷañciyattiṟku vantu cērumō?)
āḷuki, āḷutal, āṭci eṉpaṉavellām oru poruḷ tarum coṟkaḷ. ovvōr āḷum - āṇ āḷ eṉiṉum peṇ āḷ eṉiṉum - āḷukit tiṟam vāyntavar eṉpatē it tamiḻc colliṉ kuṟippupporuḷākum. āṉāl, ip poruḷ viḷaṅka vāḻntu kāṭṭiyavar viral viṭṭu eṇṇat takkavarākavē uḷḷaṉar eṉpatu varalāṟu kāṭṭum uṇmi.
ōr āṭcipparappu, nāṭāka iruntāleṉṉa? vīṭāka iruntāl eṉṉa? oru niṟuvaṉamāka iruntāl eṉṉa? ōr amippāka iruntāl eṉṉa? ataṉ ciṟappu ataṉi āḷpavar cīrmiyip poṟuttē amikiṉṟatu.
maṉṉaṉ eppaṭi, makkaḷ appaṭi eṉpatu muṭiyāṭcik kālap paḻamoḻi. āḷpavar eppaṭi, vāḻpavar appaṭi eṉpatu kuṭiyāṭcikkālap putumoḻi. mēlniliyil iruntu āṇiyiṭupavar cemmiyilarāyiṉ avarkkuk kīḻniliyil iruntu paṇipurivāriṭam cemmi taṅkumō? cemmi taṅka villi eṉak kīḻniliyil iruppāri mēlniliyil iruppār taṭṭik kēṭkavum iyalumō?
itaṉic caṅkaccāṉṟōr oruvar teḷḷitiṉ viḷakkiṉār. avar toṇṭimāṉ iḷantiriyaṉ eṉpār. āṭcippoṟuppil irunta avar āṭcipaṟṟik kūṟiya aṟivuri avar paṭṭaṟivālveḷippaṭṭatākaliṉ pōṟṟik koḷḷat takkatu eṉpatil aiyamiṉṟām.
uruḷiyiyum pāriyum kōttu ulakiṉkaṇṇē celuttum kāvaluṭiya vaṇṭi, ataṉic celuttuvōṉ māṇpuṭiyaṉ āyiṉ ūṟupāṭu illāmal vaḻiyil iṉitākac cellum. mt‹ mjid ïÅjhf¢ brY¤Jjiy¤ bjËa kh£lhjt‹ MÆ‹ mJ ehŸnjhW« gifah»¢, br¿ªj nr‰WŸ mGªâ Äf¥gy Ôa J‹g¤ij nkY« nkY« c©lh¡F«” v‹gJ mt® brhšÈa m¿îiuah«.*
kaḻaka vaṇṭiyic celuttutalilum iṭarkaḷ mikappala uṇṭāyiṉa; etiriṭṭaṉa. eṉiṉum ataṉic celuttuvōr tiṟam, ūṟupāṭukaḷi yellām aṟavē akaṟṟic cevviya cāliyil cemmāntu cella utaviyatu.
kuḻantikaḷukku eḷitil nōy paṟṟikkoḷḷal ulakaṟinta uṇmi. avvāṟē enta amippukkum toṭakkattil tollikaḷum tuyarkaḷum vantu muṭṭutal tavirkka muṭiyātavi. av vēḷikaḷil ellām āṭcip poṟuppuṭiyār tiṟaṅkaḷē amippik kaṭṭikkāttuḷḷaṉa. av vitikkuk kaḻakamum urimi pūṇṭatēyām.
1920 ām āṇṭil kaḻakam toṭaṅkiya nāḷmutal āṭcip poṟuppil iruntu arumpāṭupaṭṭavar tiruvaraṅkaṉār. akkālattil kaḻakappaṅkukaḷ cērkka avar eṭuttukkoṇṭa muyaṟcikku aḷavē illi. perumpālāṉa paṅkukaḷit tiraṭṭik kaḻakatti nilipeṟuttiya kaḻakak kāvalar avarē eṉil mikiyaṉṟām.
akkālattil iḷavalār va. cu. ceṉṉik kiḷiyiṉ mukavarāka iruntār. avar nūl veḷiyīṭṭukkākavum avaṟṟi viṟpataṟkākavum eṭuttukkoṇṭa muyaṟcikaḷ aḷavil aṭaṅkātaṉa. ivviruvaki muyaṟcikaḷum irupālum iṉitiṉ iyaṅkiyamiyāltāṉ kaḻakam taṉ kāli valimiyāka ūṉṟi nilipeṟalāyiṟṟu.
aṇṇal araṅkaṉārkkup piṉṉē 1945 ām āṇṭil āṭcippoṟuppēṟṟu nāḷitu vari naṭāttivarupavar va. cu. avarkaḷ. aṇṇal araṅkar kāval kuḻavi keḷpavar pōlak kaḻakattik kāttaṉar eṉṟāl, avarkaḷuṭaṉ aṇukkamāka iruntu aṟivaṉa aṟintu avarkaḷ vaḻittaṭattuc celvatē vāḻvāka vakuttukkoṇṭa iḷavalār va. cu. kaḻakak kuḻantikkuc celvac ceviliyāki vaḷarttup poṉṉum maṇiyum pūṭṭip poṟpuṟutti makiḻum pukaḻttoṇṭarākat tikaḻkiṟār! ippaṭit takuti vāynta āṭacimuṟiyālvāḻntu, takaviṉāl vaḷarntami yāltāṉ,. aṟiñarkku aṟiñarām aṇṇā avarkaḷ,
oru kālattil, tamiḻil ettaṉiputtakaṅkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu cilar kēṭṭatuṇṭu. āṉāl iṉṟikku ōr aiyāyiram rūpāy eṭuttuk koṇṭu civacittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakat tukkup pōṉāl, attaṉikkum tamiḻp puttakaṅkaḷ vāṅkik koṇṭu tirumpamuṭiyum. anta aḷavu tamiḻil iṉṟu puttakaṅkaḷ veḷivantirukkiṉṟaṉa.
eṉṟu pārāṭṭum tiruvāḻvu kaḻakattiṟku amintatu eṉka.
muṟkālattil oru nūli iyaṟṟiyavar annūliṉ iṟutiyil, innūlip paṭittavarum, paṭikkakkēṭṭavarum, virumpip paṭiyeṭuttuk kāttavarum, piṟarukku utaviyākat tantavarumiṉṉa iṉṉa nalaṅkaḷi yellām peṟṟup pēriṉpamum eytuvar eṉṟu eḻutuvatu uṇṭu! avvakiyil tamiḻaṉṉikku aṇikalamākiya āyirattu eḻunūṟṟimpatu nūlkaḷi aḻakoḻuka veḷiyiṭṭut tamiḻiṉ taṉiperuñ ciṟappi ulakellām parappiya kaḻakappaṇiyil īṭupaṭṭa perumakkaḷ evarāyiṉ eṉṉa, avarellām peṟṟapēṟu piṟarkku vāykkā arumiyēyām eṉṟāl, ataṉ kaṇmaṇi kaḷeṉat tikaḻnta araṅkarkkum avar iḷaval va.cu. viṟkum tamiḻakam ettuṇik kaṭamippaṭṭuḷḷatu eṉpatu collāmalē viḷaṅkum.



10. ceṉṉiyil mukavar
(māṭattiṟku eḻil, ōviyam; mānakarukkuccīr, kōpuram; nūlukku nayam, muṉṉuri; ivaṟṟip pōl oru niṟuvaṉattiṟku vaḷam, mukavar.)
tirunelvēlit teṉṉintiya civacittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakam nelliyil kālkoṇṭa nāḷilēyē ceṉṉiyilum oru kiḷiyit tōṟṟuvittatu. at tōṟṟam, tamiḻakattiṉ teṉṉelli vaṭavellikaḷi oruṅkiṇittup, paṟavikku aminta iru ciṟakukaḷ eṉa eḻuccikoṇṭu uyarntōṅka vakiyāyiṟṟu. tamiḻakac camutāya opputaṟku ēṟpat teṉpāl nelliyil iruntukoṇṭu ceytali mūttavar araṅkar mēṟkoṇṭār; vaṭapāl ceṉṉiyil iruntu vaḷarpaṇi puritali iḷiyavar va. cu. mēṟkoṇṭār. mūttavar poṟuppō, kaḻakattiṉ mēlāṇmik kaṭaṉ puriyum amiccar poṟuppu; iḷiyavar pataviyō, mukavar eṉpatu.
